Note that OPSC prints the question word as ଗଂଗା with an anusvara; the standard spelling is ଗଙ୍ଗା. Memory tip: ସ୍ୱର୍ means heaven, as in ସ୍ୱର୍ଲୋକ (the heavenly world), so ସ୍ୱର୍ଣ୍ଣଦୀ is simply 'heaven-river'.

Q 125 | odi | Vocabulary & Usage
‘ଅସୁର’ ର ପ୍ରତିଶବ୍ଦ :
(1) ତନୁଜ
(2) ଅନୁଜ
(3) ସାନୁଜ
(4) ଦନୁଜ
= 4
~ ଦନୁଜ ('born of Danu') is a synonym of ଅସୁର (demon). In the Puranas, the sage Kashyapa's wife Danu was the mother of the ଦାନବ and his wife Diti the mother of the ଦୈତ୍ୟ; both groups are called ଅସୁର, the enemies of the gods. So the synonyms of ଅସୁର include ଦାନବ, ଦନୁଜ, ଦୈତ୍ୟ, ଦିତିସୁତ, ସୁରାରି and ଦେବାରି ('enemy of the gods'). The suffix -ଜ means 'born', and the first part tells from whom or where: ତନୁଜ (born of one's body: son; feminine ତନୁଜା, daughter), ଅନୁଜ (born after: younger brother), ଅଗ୍ରଜ (born before: elder brother), ଜଳଜ and ପଙ୍କଜ (born in water or mud: lotus) and ଦ୍ୱିଜ (twice-born). ସାନୁଜ is ସ + ଅନୁଜ, 'together with the younger brother', as when Rama went to the forest ସାନୁଜ, with Lakshmana. So options A, B and C, though they look alike, mean son, younger brother and 'with the younger brother'. Trap: all four options end in -ନୁଜ, so split each word before choosing. Memory tip: ଦନୁ → ଦାନବ → ଦନୁଜ.

Q 126 | odi | Vocabulary & Usage
‘ଶିବ’ର ପ୍ରତିଶବ୍ଦ :
(1) ଶ୍ରୀଖଣ୍ଡ
(2) ଶ୍ରୀକଣ୍ଠ
(3) ଶ୍ରୀକାନ୍ତ
(4) ଶ୍ରୀଧର
= 2
~ ଶ୍ରୀକଣ୍ଠ is a name of Shiva. It means 'one whose throat bears a beautiful mark', referring to the dark-blue stain left when Shiva drank the poison ହଳାହଳ that rose during the churning of the ocean; hence his other names ନୀଳକଣ୍ଠ and ଶିତିକଣ୍ଠ. The Amarakosha lists ଶ୍ରୀକଣ୍ଠ among Shiva's names, with ଶମ୍ଭୁ, ଶଙ୍କର, ମହେଶ୍ୱର, ପିନାକୀ and ଚନ୍ଦ୍ରଶେଖର. The other options are traps built on ଶ୍ରୀ. ଶ୍ରୀଖଣ୍ଡ means sandalwood (ଚନ୍ଦନ); note that it differs from ଶ୍ରୀକଣ୍ଠ only in ଖଣ୍ଡ versus କଣ୍ଠ. ଶ୍ରୀକାନ୍ତ ('beloved of Shri', that is Lakshmi) and ଶ୍ରୀଧର ('bearer of Shri') are names of Vishnu, as are ଶ୍ରୀପତି and ଶ୍ରୀନିବାସ. Rule of thumb: names that join ଶ୍ରୀ (Lakshmi) to a word for husband or bearer belong to Vishnu, while names built on the throat, the moon, the trident or the matted hair belong to Shiva. Odisha link: Bhubaneswar's Lingaraj temple, the heart of Ekamra Kshetra, is the state's most famous Shiva shrine, and Lingaraj is worshipped there as Harihara, a joint form of Shiva and Vishnu. Memory tip: କଣ୍ଠ (throat) → poison → Shiva.

Q 127 | odi | Vocabulary & Usage
‘ମେଘ’ର ପ୍ରତିଶବ୍ଦ :
(1) ଜଳଦ
(2) ଜଳଧି
(3) ଜଳଜ
(4) ଜଗର
= 1
~ ଜଳଦ ('giver of water': ଜଳ + ଦ, from the root ଦା, 'to give') is a synonym of ମେଘ (cloud). Many cloud words follow this 'water + giver' or 'water + holder' pattern: ଜଳଦ, ବାରିଦ, ନୀରଦ, ପୟୋଦ, ଅମ୍ବୁଦ and ତୋୟଦ (all 'water-giver'), and ଜଳଧର and ବାରିଧର ('water-holder'); other synonyms are ଘନ, ଜୀମୂତ and ବଳାହକ. The distractors share the same first part, so the ending decides the meaning. ଜଳଧି (ଜଳ + ଧି, 'store of water') means the ocean, like ବାରିଧି, ପୟୋଧି and ଉଦଧି. ଜଳଜ (ଜଳ + ଜ, 'born in water') means the lotus. ଜଗର does not mean cloud; it only looks familiar because of ଅଜଗର (python), which is really ଅଜ + ଗର, 'goat-swallower'. Memory tip: -ଦ gives (cloud), -ଧି stores (ocean), -ଜ is born (lotus), -ଧର holds (cloud). Literary link: in Kalidasa's Meghaduta, an exiled yaksha sends a message to his wife through a cloud, and Odia and Sanskrit poems use these cloud names freely, so knowing the pattern helps with unseen words too. Trap: do not confuse ଜଳଧର (cloud) with ଜଳଧି (ocean); they differ by just one letter.

Q 128 | odi | Vocabulary & Usage
‘ଆକାଶ’ର ପ୍ରତିଶବ୍ଦ :
(1) ପୁଷ୍କଳ
(2) ପୁଷ୍କର
(3) ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣକ
(4) ପୁଷ୍ଟିକା
= 2
~ ପୁଷ୍କର is a tatsama (Sanskrit) word with three main meanings — sky, water and lotus — so here it is the synonym of ଆକାଶ. The classical Sanskrit word-list Amarakosha places puṣkara among the names of the sky, along with vyoma, ambara and nabha. The other options are traps built on look-alike spellings: ପୁଷ୍କଳ (ending in ଳ, not ର) means plentiful or abundant, as in ପୁଷ୍କଳ ଶସ୍ୟ (a plentiful harvest); ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣକ is linked to ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ (full); and ପୁଷ୍ଟିକା is linked to ପୁଷ୍ଟି (nourishment). None of them means sky. Other synonyms of ଆକାଶ worth learning for the exam are ଗଗନ, ନଭ, ଅମ୍ବର, ବ୍ୟୋମ, ଅନ୍ତରୀକ୍ଷ, ଆସମାନ and ଶୂନ୍ୟ. Because ପୁଷ୍କର also means water and lotus, the holy town of Pushkar in Rajasthan, famous for its lake and Brahma temple, carries the same word. Memory tip: ପୁଷ୍କର ends in ର like ଅମ୍ବର and means sky; ପୁଷ୍କଳ ends in ଳ and means plenty.

Q 129 | odi | Vocabulary & Usage
‘ବାଟ’ର ପ୍ରତିଶବ୍ଦ :
(1) ତରଣୀ
(2) ବରଣୀ
(3) ପରଣ
(4) ସରଣୀ
= 4
~ ସରଣୀ means a road, path or track along which one moves, so it is the synonym of ବାଟ. It comes from the Sanskrit root √ସୃ ('to move, to flow'), the same root seen in ସରିତ୍ (a river, which flows) and ସଂସାର. Among the distractors, ତରଣୀ means a boat: it comes from √ତୃ ('to cross'), because a boat takes you across water (in poetry ତରଣି can also mean the sun). ବରଣୀ and ପରଣ are rhyming look-alikes and do not mean a road. ବାଟ itself is a tadbhava word that grew out of Sanskrit ବର୍ତ୍ମ (path). Learn the full set of synonyms of ବାଟ for the exam: ପଥ, ମାର୍ଗ, ରାସ୍ତା, ସଡ଼କ, ବର୍ତ୍ମ, ଅଧ୍ୱ, ପନ୍ଥା, ପଦବୀ and ସରଣୀ. Trap to avoid: ସରଣୀ and ତରଣୀ differ only in the first letter — ସ for the road you walk on, ତ for the boat that carries you across the river.

Q 130 | odi | Vocabulary & Usage
‘ନୌକା’ର ପ୍ରତିଶବ୍ଦ :
(1) ବହଚି
(2) ବହୁଧା
(3) ବହିତ୍ର
(4) ବାଦିତ୍ର
= 3
~ ବହିତ୍ର means a boat or ship, so it is the synonym of ନୌକା. It comes from the Sanskrit root √ବହ୍ ('to carry') with the suffix -ଇତ୍ର, so it literally means 'that which carries' people and goods across water. The Odia word ବୋଇତ — the large trading ships of ancient Kalinga, remembered every Kartika Purnima in Boita Bandana and at Cuttack's Bali Jatra — is traced to this same word. The other options are look-alikes: ବହୁଧା means 'in many ways' (an indeclinable), ବାଦିତ୍ର means a musical instrument, and ବହଚି is not a word for a boat. Other synonyms of ନୌକା: ତରୀ, ତରଣୀ, ନାଆ, ଡଙ୍ଗା, ଜଳଯାନ and ପୋତ (a large ship). Memory tip: ବହିତ୍ର 'carries' (ବହେ), while ବାଦିତ୍ର 'plays' (ବାଜେ); both end in -ଇତ୍ର, so read the first syllable carefully before you choose.

Q 131 | odi | Grammar
ଯେଉଁ କ୍ରିୟାପଦରୁ କାଳ, ପୁରୁଷ ଓ ବଚନ ପ୍ରଭୃତିର ଧାରଣା ମିଳେ, ତାହା ______ କ୍ରିୟା ।
(1) ମିଶ୍ର କ୍ରିୟା
(2) ସିଦ୍ଧ କ୍ରିୟା
(3) ସାଧ୍ୟ କ୍ରିୟା
(4) ସମାପିକା କ୍ରିୟା
= 3
~ OPSC's key calls a verb form that tells us the tense, person and number a ସାଧ୍ୟ କ୍ରିୟା. The idea comes from the traditional Sanskrit-based analysis of action. When an action is shown as being carried out by a doer at a particular time — ଖାଉଛି, ଗଲେ, କରିବୁ — it is ସାଧ୍ୟ ('still to be accomplished'), and only such inflected forms carry tense, person and number. When the same action is named as a finished thing or idea, as in the verbal nouns ଖାଇବା, ଗମନ or ପାକ, it is ସିଦ୍ଧ ('accomplished'), and no tense, person or number can be read from it, so (B) is the opposite. ମିଶ୍ର କ୍ରିୟା is a compound verb such as ଖାଇଦେଲା, a different classification. ସମାପିକା କ୍ରିୟା, the verb that completes a sentence (ସେ ଘରକୁ ଗଲା), does also show tense, person and number, which is why some sources prefer (D); OPSC's key, however, follows the ସାଧ୍ୟ–ସିଦ୍ଧ division. Memory tip: ସାଧ୍ୟ = action in progress, with endings; ସିଦ୍ଧ = action named as a noun. (Official key; some sources differ.)

Q 132 | odi | Grammar
‘ଣି’ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟୟ ______ କାଳ ସହିତ ସଂଯୁକ୍ତ ହୋଇପାରେ ନାହିଁ ।
(1) ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନ କାଳ
(2) ଅତୀତ କାଳ
(3) ଭବିଷ୍ୟତ କାଳ
(4) କେଉଁଟି ନୁହଁ
= 1
~ The suffix ଣି adds the sense 'already, by now' to a verb, so it naturally joins completed (past) forms: ସେ ଗଲାଣି (he has already gone), ମୁଁ ଖାଇଲିଣି (I have already eaten), ରାତି ହେଲାଣି (it is night already). It can also join future forms, where it gives a sense of likelihood: ଏତେବେଳକୁ ସେ ପହଞ୍ଚିଥିବଣି (he must have reached by now). Since ଣି is used with both the past and the future, options (B) and (C) are wrong, and (D) 'none of these' is wrong too. As per OPSC's key, it is not attached to present-tense forms such as ଖାଏ or ଖାଉଛି, so the answer is ବର୍ତ୍ତମାନ କାଳ. Spelling note: it is written with the retroflex ଣ and joined to the verb (ଗଲାଣି). Don't confuse it with the negative ନି of spoken Odia, as in ଯାଇନି (= ଯାଇନାହିଁ, has not gone). A linked question in the same paper asks what ଣି expresses in a future verb; the answer there is ସମ୍ଭାବନା (possibility).

Q 135 | odi | Grammar
ଅତୀତ ଓ ଭବିଷ୍ୟତ କାଳରେ ______ ସହାୟକ କ୍ରିୟାପଦ ବ୍ୟବହାର କରାଯାଇଥାଏ ।
(1) ଅଛ୍
(2) ଥା
(3) ହେଲା
(4) ହେବା
= 2
~ Odia builds its continuous and perfect tenses with a helping verb, and the helper changes with the tense. In the present tense it is ଅଛ୍: କରୁଅଛି → କରୁଛି (is doing), କରିଅଛି → କରିଛି (has done). But ଅଛ୍ has only present forms, so in the past and future tenses ଥା takes its place: କରୁଥିଲା (was doing), କରିଥିଲା (had done), କରୁଥିବ (will be doing), କରିଥିବ (will have done). Hence ଥା. Option (A) ଅଛ୍ is the present-tense helper only; ହେଲା is the past form of the verb ହେ (to become), and ହେବା is its verbal noun, so neither is the tense auxiliary. Quick check: change ସେ ପଢ଼ୁଛି into the past and you get ସେ ପଢ଼ୁଥିଲା; the ଅଛ୍ part is replaced by a form of ଥା. Memory tip: ଅଛ୍ for ଏବେ (now), ଥା for ଥିଲା and ଥିବ (was and will be).

Q 136 | odi | Grammar
‘ପଚାରିବା’ ଧାତୁ ମୂଳ ସଂସ୍କୃତ ______ ଧାତୁରୁ ଆସିଛି ।
(1) ପ୍ରଚ୍
(2) ପୃଚ୍ଛ୍
(3) ପୃଚ୍
(4) ପ୍ରଚ୍ଛ୍
= 4
~ The Odia verb ପଚାରିବା (to ask) goes back to the Sanskrit root ପ୍ରଚ୍ଛ୍ (prach, 'to ask, to enquire'). The root is written with ର-ଫଳା on ପ and the conjunct ଚ୍ଛ. In Sanskrit its ର changes to ଋ in the present tense, giving ପୃଚ୍ଛତି (he asks), so ପୃଚ୍ଛ୍ (B) is only the present stem, not the root itself. ପ୍ରଚ୍ (A) and ପୃଚ୍ (C) are wrong because they drop the ଛ of the conjunct. The same root gives words students meet often: ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ (question), ପୃଚ୍ଛା (enquiry), and Hindi पूछना. A word that comes from Sanskrit with sound changes along the way is called tadbhava; ପଚାରିବା is one, while ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ is used unchanged as a tatsama. Memory tip: ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ and ପ୍ରଚ୍ଛ୍ both begin with ପ୍ର — the question and the act of asking come from one root, so look for the ର-ଫଳା and the ଚ୍ଛ together.

Q 137 | odi | Grammar
ମୂଳ ସଂସ୍କୃତ ‘ଲିମ୍ପ୍’ ଧାତୁରୁ ଉତ୍ପନ୍ନ ଓଡ଼ିଆ ଧାତୁଟି ______ ।
(1) ଲମ୍ଫ ମାରିବା
(2) ଲଂଘନ କରିବା
(3) ଲିପିବା
(4) ଲେଖିବା
= 3
~ The Sanskrit root √ଲିପ୍ means 'to smear, to coat, to plaster'; in the present tense it takes a nasal and becomes ଲିମ୍ପ (limpati, he smears). From it comes the Odia verb ଲିପିବା — to plaster or smear a floor or wall, as in ଗୋବରରେ ଘର ଲିପିବା, still done in Odia villages before festivals such as Manabasa Gurubar in Margasira. Related words from the same root include ଲେପ, ଲେପନ, ବିଲେପନ and ଲିପ୍ତ (smeared; also 'involved in'). The other options come from different sources: ଲେଖିବା from √ଲିଖ୍ (to write), which is the main trap because ଲିଖ୍ and ଲିପ୍ look alike; ଲଂଘନ କରିବା from √ଲଙ୍ଘ୍ (to cross over, to violate); and ଲମ୍ଫ ମାରିବା means to jump, which has nothing to do with smearing. Memory tip: ଲିମ୍ପ → ଲିପ୍ → ଲେପ → ଲିପିବା — every link in the chain is about smearing or coating.

Q 139 | odi | Grammar
‘ଯା’ ଏକ ______ ଧାତୁ ।
(1) ଅପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣରୂପ
(2) ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣରୂପ
(3) ମିଶ୍ର
(4) ଅମିଶ୍ର
= 1
~ ଯା (to go) is an ଅପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣରୂପ (defective) root because it cannot make all its tense forms by itself. Its present and future come from ଯା — ଯାଏ, ଯାଉଛି, ଯିବ — but its simple past is borrowed from another root: ଗଲା, ଗଲି, ଗଲେ, which go back to Sanskrit √ଗମ୍. English shows the same thing in go → went. Another common Odia example is ଅଛ୍, which has only present forms (ଅଛି, ଅଛନ୍ତି) and takes ଥିଲା and ଥିବ from ଥା for the past and future. A ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣରୂପ (complete) root such as ଖା, କର୍ or ପଢ଼୍ builds every tense from itself — ଖାଏ, ଖାଇଲା, ଖାଇବ — so (B) is wrong. ମିଶ୍ର and ଅମିଶ୍ର belong to a different classification, of compound verbs (ଖାଇଦେଲା) and simple verbs (ଖାଇଲା), so they do not describe this feature. Memory tip: if a root has to borrow a tense from another root, it is ଅପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ.

Q 140 | odi | Grammar
କ୍ରିୟା ବିଭକ୍ତି କହିଲେ ______ ବୁଝାଏ ।
(1) କ୍ରିୟାପଦ କେଉଁ ପୁରୁଷ ଓ ବଚନରେ ରହିଛି
(2) କ୍ରିୟାପଦ କେଉଁ କାଳରେ ରହିଛି
(3) କ୍ରିୟାପଦ କେଉଁ ଲିଙ୍ଗକୁ ସୂଚାଉଛି
(4) କ୍ରିୟାପଦ ବାକ୍ୟର କେଉଁ ପଦକୁ ବିଭକ୍ତ କରୁଛି
= 1
~ A verb's ବିଭକ୍ତି is the ending added at the very end of the verb, after the root and the tense marker, and it shows the person (ପୁରୁଷ) and number (ବଚନ) of the subject. See it in the present continuous of ଖା: ମୁଁ ଖାଉଛି (ଇ), ଆମେ ଖାଉଛୁ (ଉ), ତୁମେ ଖାଉଛ (ଅ), ସେମାନେ ଖାଉଛନ୍ତି (ଅନ୍ତି). The root ଖା and the tense marker ଉଛ୍ stay the same; only the ending changes with the subject. Option (B) is wrong because tense is shown by separate tense suffixes — ଉଛ୍ (present continuous), ଇଲ୍ (past), ଇବ୍ (future) — not by the ବିଭକ୍ତି. Option (C) is wrong because Odia verbs do not change for gender: ସେ ଖାଉଛି is the same for a boy and a girl, unlike Hindi खाता/खाती. Option (D) is meaningless, since the ending does not 'divide' any word of the sentence. Memory tip: root = meaning, tense marker = time, ବିଭକ୍ତି = who and how many.

Q 141 | odi | Grammar
‘ଖାଉଛି’ କ୍ରିୟାପଦରେ ‘ଇ’ ବିଭକ୍ତି ______ କୁ ସୂଚାଉଛି ।
(1) ପୁରୁଷ
(2) ପୁରୁଷ ଓ ବଚନ
(3) ବଚନ
(4) କେଉଁଟି ନୁହଁ
= 2
~ Split the verb: ଖାଉଛି = ଖା (root, 'eat') + ଉଛ୍ (present-continuous tense marker) + ଇ (ବିଭକ୍ତି). In Odia a single verb ending always carries person and number together; there is no separate ending for person alone or for number alone. Here ଇ goes with a singular subject of the first or third person — ମୁଁ ଖାଉଛି (I am eating), ସେ ଖାଉଛି (he or she is eating). Change the subject and the ending changes for both features at once: ଆମେ ଖାଉଛୁ (first person plural, ଉ), ସେମାନେ ଖାଉଛନ୍ତି (third person plural, ଅନ୍ତି). So (B) ପୁରୁଷ ଓ ବଚନ is right; (A) and (C) each give only half of what the ending does, and (D) is wrong because the ending clearly marks something. Trap: tense is shown not by ଇ but by ଉଛ୍. Similar example: in ସେ ପଢ଼ିଲା, the ending ଆ after the past marker ଇଲ୍ shows third person singular.

Q 142 | odi | Grammar
‘ଖାଇଦେଇ ଯାଆନ୍ତୁ’ରେ ______ ଧାତୁ ରହିଛି ।
(1) ତିନୋଟି
(2) ଦୁଇ
(3) ଏକ
(4) ଚାରୋଟି
= 1
~ Break the phrase into its parts: ଖାଇ = ଖା (eat) + ଇ, ଦେଇ = ଦେ (give) + ଇ, and ଯାଆନ୍ତୁ = ଯା (go) + ଅନ୍ତୁ. So ଖାଇଦେଇ ଯାଆନ୍ତୁ ('please eat and then go') contains three roots — ଖା, ଦେ and ଯା — and the answer is ତିନୋଟି. The first two are non-finite (ଅସମାପିକା) forms ending in ଇ; only the last, ଯାଆନ୍ତୁ, is finite, and it is the polite imperative (ଅନୁଜ୍ଞା) used for a respected person. Here ଦେ does not literally mean 'give'; it works as a helping root that adds a sense of completing the action (ଖାଇଦେବା = to eat up). Students who count only the main-meaning verbs (ଖା and ଯା), or who count the written words, get two — both are traps. Count every root, including the helper. Similar example: ପଢ଼ିସାରି ଶୋଇଗଲା has four roots — ପଢ଼୍, ସାର୍, ଶୋ and ଯା (ଗଲା being the past form of ଯା).

Q 143 | odi | Grammar
ମିଶ୍ରକ୍ରିୟାକୁ ଅମିଶ୍ରକ୍ରିୟାରେ ପରିଣତ କରାଯାଇପାରେ କି ?
(1) ନା
(2) କେତୋଟି କ୍ଷେତ୍ରରେ
(3) ହଁ
(4) କେଉଁଟି ନୁହଁ
= 3
~ A ମିଶ୍ର (compound) verb is made of a main verb in its non-finite ଇ form plus a helping root that adds a shade of meaning — ଖାଇଦେଲା, ଚାଲିଗଲା, ପଡ଼ିଗଲା, ମରିଗଲା. An ଅମିଶ୍ର (simple) verb has just one root — ଖାଇଲା, ଚାଲିଲା, ପଡ଼ିଲା, ମଲା. Because the main meaning lies in the first root, the helper can be dropped and the tense ending put on the main root itself, turning the compound into a simple verb: ସେ ଭାତ ଖାଇଦେଲା → ସେ ଭାତ ଖାଇଲା. The basic sense remains; only the extra shade of completeness or suddenness given by the helper is lost. So, as per OPSC's key, the answer is ହଁ (yes). Option (A) ନା is wrong for that reason, and the key does not take (B) 'in some cases', since the rule is stated generally; (D) is ruled out once (C) fits. Similar example: ସେ ଘରୁ ବାହାରିଗଲା → ସେ ଘରୁ ବାହାରିଲା. Tip: drop the helper (ଦେ, ଯା, ପଡ଼୍, ନେ) and keep the first root.

Q 144 | odi | Grammar
‘ସେ ଖାଉଥିବ’ ରେ କ୍ରିୟାପଦଟି ______ ଅର୍ଥସୂଚକ।
(1) ସମାପିକା ଓ ସଂପନ୍ନ
(2) ଅସମାପିକା ଓ ସଂପନ୍ନ
(3) ଅସମାପିକା ଓ ଅସଂପନ୍ନ
(4) ସମାପିକା ଓ ଅସଂପନ୍ନ
= 4
~ Two questions decide this. First, does the verb finish the sentence? In ସେ ଖାଉଥିବ the verb carries tense, person and number and the sentence is complete, so it is ସମାପିକା (finite), not ଅସମାପିକା like ଖାଇ or ଖାଉ ଖାଉ. Second, is the action shown as finished? ଖାଉଥିବ = ଖା + ଉ + ଥିବ; the ଉ marks an action still going on ('he will be eating' or 'he must be eating'), so it is ଅସଂପନ୍ନ (incomplete, continuous). Compare ସେ ଖାଇଥିବ, where the ଇ marks a finished action ('he will have eaten'); that would be ସମାପିକା ଓ ସଂପନ୍ନ, option (A). Options (B) and (C) fail because the verb is not non-finite. Memory tip: in these ଥା forms, ଉ before ଥା = still going on (ଖାଉଥିଲା, ଖାଉଥିବ), and ଇ before ଥା = already done (ଖାଇଥିଲା, ଖାଇଥିବ). This ଉ/ଇ test works for the past and the future alike.

Q 145 | odi | Grammar
‘ଭୁଲି ଯାଇଥିବ’ କ୍ରିୟାପଦର ______ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟୟଟି ଭବିଷ୍ୟତକାଳ ସୂଚକ ।
(1) ଯାଇଥିବ
(2) ଥିବ
(3) ଇବ
(4) ବ
= 3
~ Break the verb into its parts: ଭୁଲି ଯାଇଥିବ = ଭୁଲ୍ + ଇ (non-finite) + ଯା + ଇ (non-finite) + ଥା + ଇବ. When the future suffix ଇବ joins ଥା, the vowel ଆ drops and we get ଥିବ. The part that marks future time is therefore ଇବ — the same suffix seen in କରିବ, ଦେଖିବ and ଖାଇବ, and, with person endings, in କରିବି (I will do) and କରିବେ (they will do). Options (A) ଯାଇଥିବ and (B) ଥିବ are whole verb forms containing a root plus the suffix, not the suffix itself; option (D) ବ is only a fragment, because the ଇ of the suffix stays clearly visible in forms like ଖାଇବ. Compare the other tense suffixes: ଉଛ୍ for the present continuous (ଖାଉଛି) and ଇଲ୍ for the past (ଖାଇଲା). Memory tip: ଇଲ୍ = was (ଅତୀତ), ଇବ୍ = will (ଭବିଷ୍ୟତ). When splitting any verb, peel off the person ending first; what is left before it is the tense suffix.

Q 146 | odi | Grammar
ଅସମାପିକା କ୍ରିୟାପଦରେ ______ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟୟ ପ୍ରୟୋଗ କରାଯାଏ ।
(1) ଅନ୍ତି
(2) ଅ
(3) ଉଛି
(4) ଇ
= 4
~ An ଅସମାପିକା (non-finite) verb does not finish the sentence and shows no person or number; it links one action to the next. The commonest way to form it in Odia is to add the suffix ଇ to the root: ଖା + ଇ = ଖାଇ, ଦେଖ୍ + ଇ = ଦେଖି, ଶୁଣ୍ + ଇ = ଶୁଣି, as in ସେ ଭାତ ଖାଇ ସ୍କୁଲ ଗଲା (having eaten, he went to school). Here ଖାଇ is non-finite and ଗଲା is the finite verb that completes the sentence. The other options belong to finite verbs: ଅନ୍ତି is the third-person plural ending (ସେମାନେ ଖାଆନ୍ତି), ଉଛି is the present-continuous ending (ଖାଉଛି), and ଅ is the ending used with ତୁମେ (ତୁମେ ଖାଅ, ତୁମେ କର). Other non-finite forms worth knowing are ଖାଇବାକୁ (to eat) and ଖାଉ ଖାଉ (while eating). Memory tip: if an ଇ-ending verb leaves you waiting for 'and then what?', it is ଅସମାପିକା.

Q 147 | odi | Grammar
ନାମଧାତୁ କ୍ରିୟାପଦ ______ ଶବ୍ଦରୁ ଗଠିତ ହୋଇଥାଏ ।
(1) ବିଶେଷଣ
(2) ଅବ୍ୟୟ
(3) କାଳ
(4) ବଚନ
= 1
~ A ନାମଧାତୁ is a verb root that is not an original root but is made from a naming word — a noun (ବିଶେଷ୍ୟ) or an adjective (ବିଶେଷଣ) — usually by adding the verb-making ending ଆ/ଏଇ. From adjectives: ଗରମ (hot) → ଗରମେଇବା (to heat), ଚିକ୍କଣ (smooth) → ଚିକ୍କଣେଇବା (to make smooth). From nouns: ଲାଜ (shame) → ଲାଜେଇବା, ଚୋର (thief) → ଚୋରେଇବା (to steal), ଠେଙ୍ଗା (stick) → ଠେଙ୍ଗେଇବା (to beat with a stick). Among the options only ବିଶେଷଣ is a naming word of this kind, so OPSC's key gives (A). ଅବ୍ୟୟ (indeclinables such as ଓ, କିନ୍ତୁ, ହଠାତ୍) are not used to make such roots, and କାଳ (tense) and ବଚନ (number) are grammatical categories, not words at all. The name itself is the clue: ନାମ (naming word) + ଧାତୁ (root). Don't confuse ନାମଧାତୁ with ପ୍ରେରଣାର୍ଥକ verbs like କରାଇବା, which are made from a verb root, not from a noun or adjective.

Q 148 | odi | Grammar
‘ଖନେଇବା’ ଏକ ______ କ୍ରିୟାପଦ ।
(1) ପ୍ରେରଣାର୍ଥକ
(2) ନାସ୍ତ
(3) ଅମିଶ୍ର
(4) ନାମଧାତୁ
= 4
~ OPSC's key classes ଖନେଇବା as a ନାମଧାତୁ verb. A ନାମଧାତୁ is a verb made from a naming word (a noun or an adjective) by adding the verb-making ending ଏଇ/ଆଇ, not from an original verb root: ଲାଜ → ଲାଜେଇବା, ଗରମ → ଗରମେଇବା, ବାଟ → ବାଟେଇବା, ଚୋର → ଚୋରେଇବା. The key places ଖନେଇବା in this group. The other choices do not fit the key: a ପ୍ରେରଣାର୍ଥକ (causative) verb is made from a verb root to show that the subject gets someone else to act, as in କର୍ → କରାଇବା, ଖା → ଖୁଆଇବା; a ନାସ୍ତ୍ୟାର୍ଥକ verb is a negative verb such as ନୁହେଁ or ନାହିଁ; and an ଅମିଶ୍ର verb is just a simple one-root verb like ଖାଇଲା. Exam tip: when an -ଏଇବା verb appears, look at what stands before the ending. If it is a naming word (ଲାଜ, ଗରମ, ବାଟ), call it ନାମଧାତୁ; if it is a verb root whose action someone is made to perform (କର୍, ପଢ଼୍), call it ପ୍ରେରଣାର୍ଥକ.

Q 149 | odi | Grammar
ଇକାରାନ୍ତ ଅସମାପିକା କ୍ରିୟା ସହ ______ ଅସମାପିକା କ୍ରିୟା ମଧ୍ୟ ସଂଯୁକ୍ତ ହୋଇପାରେ ।
(1) କରି
(2) ଦେ
(3) ପଢ଼ି
(4) ସାରି
= 1
~ An ଇ-ending (ଇକାରାନ୍ତ) non-finite verb such as ଦେଖି, ଶୁଣି, ଜାଣି or ଖାଇ can be followed by another non-finite verb, କରି, with no change in meaning: ଦେଖି କରି = ଦେଖି, ଶୁଣି କରି = ଶୁଣି, ଜାଣି କରି = ଜାଣି (knowingly). For example, ସେ ସବୁ କଥା ଶୁଣି କରି ଚାଲିଗଲା means the same as ସେ ସବୁ କଥା ଶୁଣି ଚାଲିଗଲା. The extra କରି, itself the ଇ-form of କର୍, only adds emphasis or rhythm, and this is the pattern the rule describes; so OPSC's key gives (A). ଦେ (B) is a bare root, not a non-finite form at all. ପଢ଼ି (C) is an ordinary ଇ-form verb with its own meaning; it does not attach to another one in this way. ସାରି (D) can follow an ଇ-form (ଖାଇସାରି), but it adds a new meaning — 'having finished' — and makes a compound verb, so it is not the case this rule describes. Memory tip: କରି is the 'free add-on' — remove it and nothing changes.

Q 150 | odi | Grammar
‘ଅନ୍ତି’ କ୍ରିୟା ବିଭକ୍ତିରୁ ______ ରୂପ ଜଣାପଡେ ।
(1) ପ୍ରଥମ ପୁରୁଷ ଏକବଚନ
(2) ତୃତୀୟ ପୁରୁଷ ବହୁବଚନ
(3) ତୃତୀୟ ପୁରୁଷ ଏକବଚନ
(4) ଦ୍ୱିତୀୟ ପୁରୁଷ ବହୁବଚନ
= 2
~ The verb ending ଅନ୍ତି goes with a third-person plural subject: ସେମାନେ ଖାଆନ୍ତି (they eat), ପିଲାମାନେ ଖେଳନ୍ତି (the children play), ସେମାନେ କରନ୍ତି. The same ending is also used for one person spoken of with respect, because Odia uses the plural to show honour: ବାପା ଆସନ୍ତି, ଆପଣ କେମିତି ଅଛନ୍ତି? Compare the other endings of the simple present: ମୁଁ କରେ (ଏ), ସେ କରେ (ଏ), ତୁମେ କର (ଅ), ଆମେ କରୁ (ଉ). So (A) and (C) are wrong because singular subjects take ଏ, and (D) is wrong because ତୁମେ/ତୁମେମାନେ take ଅ. ଅନ୍ତି also appears in the present continuous and present perfect — କରୁଛନ୍ତି, କରିଛନ୍ତି — but in the past and future the third-person plural takes ଏ instead (କଲେ, କରିବେ), a point often tested. Memory tip: the ନ୍ତ of ଅନ୍ତି stands for many people (or one respected person).

Q 151 | odi | Grammar
______ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟୟ ଯୋଗେ ପ୍ରେରଣାର୍ଥକ କ୍ରିୟାପଦ ଗଠିତ ହୋଇପାରେ ।
(1) ଏ
(2) ଆ
(3) ଅ
(4) ଅ, ଆ ଏବଂ ଏ
= 4
~ A ପ୍ରେରଣାର୍ଥକ (causative) verb shows that the subject makes someone else do the action: ମା ପିଲାକୁ ଖୁଆଇଲେ (the mother fed the child), ଶିକ୍ଷକ ପିଲାଙ୍କୁ ପଢ଼ାନ୍ତି (the teacher teaches the children). It is formed by adding a causal vowel to the verb root. The standard written form uses ଆ: କର୍ → କରା (କରାଇବା), ପଢ଼୍ → ପଢ଼ା (ପଢ଼ାଇବା), ଦେଖ୍ → ଦେଖା (ଦେଖାଇବା). In everyday speech the same vowel often becomes ଏ — କରେଇବା, ପଢ଼େଇବା, ଦେଖେଇବା — and it shortens to ଅ in forms such as ପଢ଼ଉଛନ୍ତି, ଦେଖଉଛି and ଗାଡ଼ି ଚଳଉଛି (= ପଢ଼ାଉଛନ୍ତି, ଦେଖାଉଛି, ଚଳାଉଛି). As per OPSC's key, all three — ଅ, ଆ and ଏ — can form causatives, so (D) is right and each single option is incomplete. Trap: a causative is built on a verb root (କର୍, ପଢ଼୍), while a ନାମଧାତୁ such as ଲାଜେଇବା is built on a noun or adjective, even though both may end in -ଏଇବା. Tip: if 'make someone…' fits the meaning, it is ପ୍ରେରଣାର୍ଥକ.

Q 152 | odi | Grammar
______ ଏକ ନାସ୍ତ୍ୟାର୍ଥକ କ୍ରିୟା ।
(1) ନ
(2) ନୁହେଁ
(3) ନା
(4) କେଉଁଟି ନୁହେଁ
= 2
~ ନୁହେଁ ('is not') is itself a verb with a negative meaning, so it is a ନାସ୍ତ୍ୟାର୍ଥକ (negative) verb: ଏହା ମୋର ବହି ନୁହେଁ (this is not my book). The proof that it is a verb is that it changes with the person and number of the subject, like any other verb: ସେ ନୁହେଁ, ତୁମେ ନୁହଁ, ସେମାନେ ନୁହଁନ୍ତି. ନାହିଁ ('is not there, does not exist') is the other negative verb in Odia: ସେ ଘରେ ନାହିଁ, ସେମାନେ ନାହାନ୍ତି. By contrast, ନ and ନା are only negative particles (ଅବ୍ୟୟ). They never change form and simply negate another verb or answer a question: ସେ ନ ଖାଇ ଚାଲିଗଲା (he left without eating), ନା, ମୁଁ ଯିବି ନାହିଁ. So (A) and (C) are wrong, and (D) is wrong because (B) fits. Memory tip: a negative verb can stand as the only verb of a sentence (ଏହା ସତ ନୁହେଁ), while ନ always needs another verb to lean on.

Q 153 | odi | Grammar
______ ଏକ କ୍ରିୟାବିଶେଷଣ ପଦ ।
(1) କୁଳୁକୁଳୁ ଝରୁଛି
(2) ଘୁମନ୍ତ ଆକାଶ
(3) ସୁଲୁସୁଲୁ ବାଆ
(4) କୁଳୁକୁଳୁ ଝରଣା
= 1
~ A କ୍ରିୟାବିଶେଷଣ (adverb) describes a verb — how, when or where an action happens. In କୁଳୁକୁଳୁ ଝରୁଛି ('is flowing with a gurgling sound'), the sound-word କୁଳୁକୁଳୁ tells us how the water ଝରୁଛି (is flowing), so it qualifies a verb and is an adverb; hence (A). In the other three options the first word describes a noun, so it is a ବିଶେଷଣ (adjective): ଘୁମନ୍ତ ଆକାଶ (the sleeping sky — ଘୁମନ୍ତ is a participle used as an adjective), ସୁଲୁସୁଲୁ ବାଆ (a gentle breeze; ବାଆ means wind) and କୁଳୁକୁଳୁ ଝରଣା (a murmuring stream). Option (D) is the real trap: the same word କୁଳୁକୁଳୁ is an adverb in (A) but an adjective in (D), because a word's class depends on what it qualifies, not on the word itself. Odia is rich in such doubled sound-words (ଧ୍ୱନ୍ୟାତ୍ମକ ଶବ୍ଦ): ଟପଟପ, ଝରଝର, ଧଡ଼ଧଡ଼. Similar example: ପିଲାଟି ଧୀରେ ଚାଲିଲା — ଧୀରେ qualifies ଚାଲିଲା, so it is a କ୍ରିୟାବିଶେଷଣ.

Q 154 | odi | Grammar
______ ଏକ ଅକର୍ମକ କ୍ରିୟା ।
(1) ଜଣାଇବା
(2) ବାଟେଇବା
(3) ଶୋଉଅଛି
(4) ପଛଉଛି
= 3
~ An ଅକର୍ମକ (intransitive) verb has no object; the action stays with the doer. ଶୋଉଅଛି (ଶୋ + ଉ + ଅଛି, 'is sleeping'; ଶୋଉଛି in speech) is complete without one — ପିଲାଟି ଶୋଉଅଛି — and the question 'sleeping what?' makes no sense, so it is intransitive. It comes straight from the primary root ଶୋ, which never takes an object. The other options are derived verbs: ଜଣାଇବା is the causative of ଜାଣ୍ ('to make known') and takes an object — ମୁଁ ତାଙ୍କୁ ଖବର ଜଣାଇଲି; ବାଟେଇବା is a ନାମଧାତୁ from ବାଟ, meaning to lead someone along the way, again with an object; and ପଛଉଛି is a ନାମଧାତୁ form built on ପଛ (back), which the key does not take as the intransitive answer. Quick test: put କ'ଣ? or କାହାକୁ? after the verb. If there is an answer (ଖବର ଜଣାଇଲି → what? news), the verb is ସକର୍ମକ; if not, it is ଅକର୍ମକ. Other intransitive verbs: ହସିବା, କାନ୍ଦିବା, ବସିବା, ଉଠିବା.

Q 155 | odi | Grammar
ଭବିଷ୍ୟତ କାଳିକ କ୍ରିୟାପଦରେ / ଣି/ର ପ୍ରୟୋଗ ______ ଭାବ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରେ ।
(1) ସମ୍ଭାବନା
(2) ଅନୁଜ୍ଞା
(3) ସର୍ତ୍ତ
(4) ନିଶ୍ଚିତ
= 1
~ When the particle ଣି is added to a future-tense verb, it turns a plain statement about the future into a guess about what has probably happened by now. ଏତେବେଳକୁ ସେ ପହଞ୍ଚିଥିବଣି means 'he must have reached by now' — the speaker is not sure, only supposing. So, as per OPSC's key, it expresses ସମ୍ଭାବନା (possibility). It is not ଅନୁଜ୍ଞା (command), which uses imperative forms like ଯାଅ or ଯାଆନ୍ତୁ; not ସର୍ତ୍ତ (condition), which uses ଯଦି…ତେବେ or an -ଇଲେ form like ଗଲେ ('if he goes'); and not ନିଶ୍ଚିତ (certainty), which is expressed by the plain past or by ନିଶ୍ଚୟ, as in ସେ ପହଞ୍ଚିଗଲେଣି ('he has already reached'). Recall the earlier question in this paper: ଣି ordinarily joins completed (past) forms and means 'already' (ସେ ଗଲାଣି), and, per the key, it does not join present-tense verbs. Another example of the guessing sense: ପିଲାମାନେ ଏବେ ଶୋଇଥିବେଣି ('the children must be asleep by now').

Q 156 | odi | Grammar
କ୍ରିୟା ପଦ ପୂର୍ବରୁ ______ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନକଲେ ଉତ୍ତରରେ କର୍ତ୍ତାକାରକ ମିଳିଥାଏ ।
(1) କେବେ
(2) କିଏ ବା କଅଣ
(3) କାହିଁକି
(4) କ’ଣ
= 2
~ The doer (କର୍ତ୍ତା) of a sentence is found by asking କିଏ ('who?') or କଅଣ ('what?') before the verb. କିଏ catches living doers and କଅଣ catches non-living ones: in 'ରାମ ଖେଳୁଛି', କିଏ ଖେଳୁଛି? — ରାମ; in 'ଗଛରୁ ପତ୍ର ଝଡ଼ୁଛି', କଅଣ ଝଡ଼ୁଛି? — ପତ୍ର. Both questions are needed for the rule to be complete, so (B) is right. କେବେ ('when?') brings out the time of the action, which belongs to the ଅଧିକରଣ (locative) case, as in 'ସେ ସକାଳେ ଆସିଲା'. କାହିଁକି ('why?') gives the reason or purpose, not the doer. କ’ଣ alone is only half the test: it misses living doers, and the same question often yields the object (କର୍ମ) instead, as in 'ସେ କ’ଣ ଖାଉଛି? — ଭାତ'. Memory tip for karaka questions: କର୍ତ୍ତା — କିଏ/କଅଣ; କର୍ମ — କାହାକୁ/କ’ଣ; କରଣ — କାହା ଦ୍ୱାରା/କେଉଁଥିରେ; ଅପାଦାନ — କେଉଁଠାରୁ; ଅଧିକରଣ — କେଉଁଠାରେ/କେବେ. Trap: always put the question to the verb, not to some other word in the sentence.

Q 157 | odi | Grammar
କ୍ରିୟାପଦର ମୂଳରୂପକୁ ପାରଂପରିକ ବ୍ୟାକରଣରେ ______ କୁହାଯାଏ ।
(1) ମୂଳ
(2) ପ୍ରାତିପଦିକ
(3) ଧାତୁ
(4) ପ୍ରକୃତି
= 3
~ In traditional Odia (and Sanskrit) grammar the basic, suffix-free form of a verb is called ଧାତୁ (root): କର୍, ଖା, ପଢ଼୍, ଦେଖ୍. Verb suffixes are added to it to make real verb forms, e.g. କର୍ + ଇବା = କରିବା, ପଢ଼୍ + ଇଲା = ପଢ଼ିଲା, ଦେଖ୍ + ଇ = ଦେଖି. ପ୍ରାତିପଦିକ is the base of a noun, pronoun or adjective before a case ending (ବିଭକ୍ତି) is added, e.g. ବାଳକ in ବାଳକକୁ. ପ୍ରକୃତି is the wider term for any base that takes a suffix; it has two kinds, ନାମ ପ୍ରକୃତି (the noun base, i.e. ପ୍ରାତିପଦିକ) and ଧାତୁ ପ୍ରକୃତି (the verb base), so it is not the specific name for a verb root. ମୂଳ simply means 'root, origin' in everyday language and is not the technical term. Remember the formula ପ୍ରକୃତି + ପ୍ରତ୍ୟୟ = ପଦ: for verbs the ପ୍ରକୃତି is a ଧାତୁ, for nouns it is a ପ୍ରାତିପଦିକ. A related term often tested: a root made from a noun or adjective, like ଗରମେଇବା from ଗରମ, is a ନାମଧାତୁ.

Q 158 | odi | Grammar
______ ଏକ ପାଦପୂରଣାର୍ଥକ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟୟ ନୁହେଁ ।
(1) ଣି
(2) ଟି
(3) ହେ
(4) ତ
= 1
~ ଣି is the odd one out because it carries a real meaning. Expletive or 'line-filling' (ପାଦପୂରଣାର୍ଥକ) particles such as ଟି, ହେ and ତ add no new information; they only round off a sentence, soften a request or add a touch of feeling, and the sentence means the same without them: 'ଆସ ତ' = 'ଆସ', 'ଶୁଣ ହେ' = 'ଶୁଣ'. The name tells you their job: ପାଦ (a line of verse) + ପୂରଣ (filling), because poets used such words to complete the metre of a line. ଣି is different. Added to a past-tense verb it shows that the action is already complete, as in 'ସେ ଆସିଲାଣି' ('he has come by now') or 'ମୁଁ ଖାଇଲିଣି'; added to a future form it shows likelihood, as in 'ଏତେବେଳକୁ ସେ ପହଞ୍ଚିଥିବଣି' ('he must have reached by now', see Q155). Remove ଣି and the meaning changes: 'ସେ ଆସିଲା' only says that he came. A test to remember: if dropping the particle leaves the meaning untouched, it is a filler; if the sense changes, it is a meaningful suffix.

Q 159 | odi | Grammar
କେଉଁଟି ମିଶ୍ର କ୍ରିୟାପଦ ?
(1) ଶୋଇବା
(2) ନୋହିବା
(3) ଶୁଆଇବା
(4) ଅବତରଣ କରିବା
= 4
~ ଅବତରଣ କରିବା ('to land, to descend') is a mixed verb (ମିଶ୍ର କ୍ରିୟା): it joins the noun ଅବତରଣ with the verb କରିବା, and together they express one action. Such verbs are formed from a noun, adjective or indeclinable plus a verb like କରିବା, ହେବା, ଦେବା or ପାଇବା: ସ୍ନାନ କରିବା, ଭୟ କରିବା, ରାଜି ହେବା, ଦଣ୍ଡ ଦେବା, ଲାଜ ପାଇବା. The noun part carries the meaning and the verb part carries tense and person (ବିମାନ ଅବତରଣ କଲା). The other options are single-word verbs. ଶୋଇବା is a simple, original (ମୌଳିକ) verb from the root ଶୋ. ନୋହିବା is the negative verb 'not to be' (ନ + ହେବା), still one word and not noun + verb. ଶୁଆଇବା ('to put to sleep') is the causative (ପ୍ରେରଣାର୍ଥକ) form of ଶୋଇବା. Don't mix up ମିଶ୍ର with ଯୌଗିକ: a ଯୌଗିକ verb joins two verbs (ପଡ଼ିଗଲା = ପଡ଼ି + ଗଲା, Q164), while a ମିଶ୍ର verb joins a non-verb word with a verb.

Q 160 | odi | Grammar
ଯାହା ସାହାଯ୍ୟରେ କ୍ରିୟା ସାଧିତ ହୁଏ ତାହା ______ କାରକ ।
(1) କରଣ
(2) ଅପାଦାନ
(3) ଅଧିକରଣ
(4) କର୍ତ୍ତା
= 1
~ The thing with whose help an action is done is the କରଣ କାରକ (instrumental case). In 'ସେ କଲମରେ ଲେଖୁଛି', ask 'କାହା ଦ୍ୱାରା ବା କେଉଁଥିରେ ଲେଖୁଛି?' and the answer, କଲମରେ, is the instrument. Other examples: 'ଛୁରୀରେ ଫଳ କାଟିଲା', 'ଚାବି ଦ୍ୱାରା ତାଲା ଖୋଲିଲା'. It takes the third case ending (ତୃତୀୟା ବିଭକ୍ତି), shown by markers such as ରେ, ଦ୍ୱାରା and ଦେଇ. ଅପାଦାନ is the point from which something separates or starts, marked by ରୁ/ଠାରୁ: 'ଗଛରୁ ପତ୍ର ଝଡ଼ିଲା'. ଅଧିକରଣ is the place or time of the action, marked by ରେ/ଠାରେ: 'ପୋଖରୀରେ ମାଛ ଅଛି', 'ସେ ସକାଳେ ଆସିଲା'. କର୍ତ୍ତା is the doer of the action (ସେ in the first example), not the means. Trap: ରେ appears in both କରଣ and ଅଧିକରଣ, so decide by meaning. If the word is a tool or means (କଲମରେ, ଛୁରୀରେ), it is କରଣ; if it is a place or time (ଘରେ, ରାତିରେ), it is ଅଧିକରଣ.

Q 161 | odi | Grammar
ନାସ୍ତି ଅର୍ଥ ସୂଚାଇବା ନିମନ୍ତେ ଅସମାପିକ କ୍ରିୟା ସହିତ ______ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟୟ ବ୍ୟବହାର କରାଯାଇପାରେ ।
(1) ନା
(2) ନୁହେଁ
(3) ନ
(4) ନାହିଁ
= 3
~ To make a non-finite (ଅସମାପିକା) verb negative, Odia puts ନ directly before it: ନ ଖାଇ, ନ ଯାଇ, ନ ଦେଖି, ନ ପଢ଼ି. Example: 'ସେ ନ ଖାଇ ସ୍କୁଲ ଗଲା' ('he went to school without eating'). The same ନ also negates conditional and verbal-noun forms: 'ନ ଆସିଲେ' ('if he does not come'), 'ନ ପଢ଼ିବା' ('not studying'). The other options belong elsewhere. ନାହିଁ comes after a finite (ସମାପିକା) verb, as in 'ସେ ଖାଇଲା ନାହିଁ', and also means 'is not there', as in 'ଘରେ କେହି ନାହିଁ'. ନୁହେଁ denies identity or quality and is itself a negative verb: 'ଏହା ମୋ ବହି ନୁହେଁ' (see Q152). ନା is mainly a reply word ('no') or a question tag: 'ତୁମେ ଯିବ ନା?'. Memory tip: ନ stands before the non-finite verb, ନାହିଁ stands after the finite verb. A handy check: if the verb form ends in ଇ (ଖାଇ, ଦେଖି) and cannot finish the sentence by itself, its negative is made with ନ placed in front.

Q 162 | odi | Grammar
______ ଏକ କ୍ରିୟା ବିଶେଷଣ ପଦ ।
(1) ସତକଥା
(2) ବକବକ ହେଉଛି
(3) ବକରା ମଣିଷ
(4) ଚଳନ୍ତାଗାଡ଼ି
= 2
~ In 'ବକବକ ହେଉଛି', the word ବକବକ ('chattering') tells how the action ହେଉଛି is going on, so it qualifies a verb and is a କ୍ରିୟାବିଶେଷଣ (adverb). It is a sound-imitating double word (ଧ୍ୱନ୍ୟାତ୍ମକ ଶବ୍ଦ), like କୁଳୁକୁଳୁ in 'କୁଳୁକୁଳୁ ଝରୁଛି' (Q153). In the other options the first word qualifies a noun, so it is a ବିଶେଷଣ (adjective): ସତ in ସତକଥା ('a true word') describes କଥା; ବକରା describes ମଣିଷ, telling what kind of person; and ଚଳନ୍ତା ('moving') describes ଗାଡ଼ି. ଚଳନ୍ତା is a trap: it comes from the verb ଚଳିବା, but here it works as a participle adjective (କୃଦନ୍ତ ବିଶେଷଣ) before a noun, not as an adverb. Quick test: look at the word right after the modifier. If it is a verb (ହେଉଛି, ଝରୁଛି, ଆସ), the modifier is an adverb; if it is a noun (କଥା, ମଣିଷ, ଗାଡ଼ି), the modifier is an adjective. Notice that ବକବକ and ବକରା look alike, yet one is an adverb and the other an adjective: the word's job in the sentence decides its class.

Q 163 | odi | Grammar
‘ଭୂ’ ଧାତୁରୁ ______ ଓଡ଼ିଆ କ୍ରିୟାପଦ ସୃଷ୍ଟି ।
(1) ଭାବିବା
(2) ଭାଳିବା
(3) ହାରିବା
(4) ହେବା
= 4
~ The Sanskrit root ଭୂ means 'to be, to become' (ଭବତି, 'is, becomes'). In Prakrit, ଭବତି wore down to a form beginning with ho (as in Prakrit hoi), and from this came the Odia verb ହେବା with forms such as ହୁଏ, ହେଲା, ହେବ, ହୋଇ. So ହେବା is the tadbhava (changed) descendant of ଭୂ; Hindi होना grew the same way. The root ଭୂ also survives unchanged in many tatsama words used in Odia: ଭବନ, ଭୂତ, ଭବିଷ୍ୟତ, ଅନୁଭବ, ପ୍ରଭାବ. Of the other options, ଭାବିବା ('to think') is built on the noun ଭାବ ('thought'), not taken straight from ଭୂ as a verb; ଭାଳିବା ('to worry, to ponder') and ହାରିବା ('to lose') are separate roots with no link to the sense of 'being'. Trap: don't be misled by sound. ଭାବିବା and ଭାଳିବା begin with ଭ like ଭୂ, but meaning is the real test: ଭୂ = to be or become, and the Odia verb with that meaning is ହେବା. Sound-change tip: the ଭ (bh) of ଭୂ turned into ହ (h) on the way from Sanskrit to Odia (ଭବତି → hoi → ହୁଏ).

Q 164 | odi | Grammar
‘ପଡ଼ିଗଲେ’ ______ କ୍ରିୟା ।
(1) ମୌଳିକ
(2) ଅକର୍ମକ
(3) ପ୍ରେରଣାର୍ଥକ
(4) ଯୌଗିକ
= 4
~ ପଡ଼ିଗଲେ is a compound (ଯୌଗିକ) verb because it is made of two verbs: the non-finite ପଡ଼ି ('having fallen') + the finite ଗଲେ ('went'). The first verb carries the main meaning, while the second, a helping verb, adds a sense of suddenness or completion and carries tense and person. Similar verbs: ଖାଇଦେଲା, ଶୋଇପଡ଼ିଲା, ଚାଲିଗଲା, ଲେଖିସାରିଲା. The question is about how the verb is built, so the other structural labels fail: a ମୌଳିକ (simple) verb is a single root form such as ପଡ଼ିଲେ, and a ପ୍ରେରଣାର୍ଥକ (causative) verb makes someone else act, as in ପଢ଼ାଇଲେ or ଶୁଆଇଲେ. ଅକର୍ମକ (intransitive) is a classification by object, not by structure; it is true that falling needs no object, but that is not what makes ପଡ଼ିଗଲେ special, and OPSC's key takes ଯୌଗିକ. Tip: if you see an ଇ-ending non-finite form (ପଡ଼ି, ଖାଇ, ଚାଲି) followed by ଗଲା, ଦେଲା, ପଡ଼ିଲା, ସାରିଲା or ପକାଇଲା, it is a ଯୌଗିକ verb. Compare Q166, which asks about the form of its first part.

Q 165 | odi | Grammar
ପ୍ରେରଣାର୍ଥକ କ୍ରିୟାପଦରେ ______ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟୟ ସଂଯୁକ୍ତ ହୋଇଥାଏ ।
(1) ଉ
(2) ଏ
(3) ଇ
(4) ଣି
= 2
~ Only ଏ among the options can make a causative (ପ୍ରେରଣାର୍ଥକ) verb, one in which the subject makes someone else do the action. In everyday Odia the causative takes ଏ: କର୍ → କରେଇବା, ପଢ଼୍ → ପଢ଼େଇବା, ଦେଖ୍ → ଦେଖେଇବା, ହସ୍ → ହସେଇବା. Standard written Odia uses ଆ for the same verbs (କରାଇବା, ପଢ଼ାଇବା, ଦେଖାଇବା), which is why Q151's key lists ଅ, ଆ and ଏ together; here only ଏ is offered. Example: 'ଶିକ୍ଷକ ପିଲାମାନଙ୍କୁ ପଢ଼େଇଲେ' — the teacher is not reading; he makes the children read. The other choices do different jobs: ଇ makes the non-finite 'having done' form (କରି, ଖାଇ; Q146); ଉ appears in continuous forms (ଖାଉଛି, କରୁ କରୁ); and ଣି is a particle of completion or likelihood (ସେ ଆସିଲାଣି; Q155, Q158). Test for a causative: look for two doers, one who causes the action (ଶିକ୍ଷକ) and one who actually does it (ପିଲାମାନେ). Tip: a causative often turns an intransitive verb into a transitive one, as in ଶୋଇବା ('sleep') → ଶୁଆଇବା ('put to sleep') and ହସିବା ('laugh') → ହସେଇବା ('make laugh').

Q 166 | odi | Grammar
ଯୌଗିକ କ୍ରିୟାପଦର ପ୍ରଥମ କ୍ରିୟାଟି ______ ରୂପ ଗ୍ରହଣ କରେ ।
(1) ଅସମାପିକା
(2) ଗୌଣ
(3) ସମାପିକା
(4) ମିଶ୍ର
= 1
~ In a compound (ଯୌଗିକ) verb the first verb always stands in its non-finite (ଅସମାପିକା) form, usually ending in ଇ, and only the second verb is finite (ସମାପିକା), carrying tense, person and number. In ପଡ଼ିଗଲା = ପଡ଼ି + ଗଲା and ଖାଇଦେଲା = ଖାଇ + ଦେଲା, the first parts ପଡ଼ି and ଖାଇ cannot end a sentence on their own; ଗଲା and ଦେଲା complete it. Change the tense and only the second part changes: ଖାଇଦେବ, ଖାଇଦେଉଛି, while ଖାଇ stays the same. Why the others are wrong: ସମାପିକା is the form of the second verb, not the first; ମିଶ୍ର is a type of verb (noun + verb, as in ଅବତରଣ କରିବା, Q159), not a form that one part of a verb takes; and ଗୌଣ ('secondary') is not a verb form at all. If anything, it is the second, helping verb that plays the minor role, while the first verb carries the main meaning. Tip: ଅସମାପିକା = 'incomplete' (it cannot finish a sentence), ସମାପିକା = 'completing'. In a ଯୌଗିକ verb the pattern is always ଅସମାପିକା + ସମାପିକା.

Q 167 | odi | Grammar
‘ହୁଗୁଳେଇବା’ କ୍ରିୟାପଦଟି ______ ପଦରୁ ଗଠିତ।
(1) ବିଶେଷଣ
(2) ବିଶେଷ୍ୟ
(3) ସର୍ବନାମ
(4) ବଚନ
= 1
~ ହୁଗୁଳେଇବା ('to loosen') is a nominal verb (ନାମଧାତୁ): it is made not from an original verb root but from the adjective ହୁଗୁଳା ('loose'), by adding the verb-making ଏଇ ending: ହୁଗୁଳା → ହୁଗୁଳେଇବା, as in 'ଦଉଡ଼ି ହୁଗୁଳେଇ ଦିଅ' ('loosen the rope'). So its source word is a ବିଶେଷଣ. Odia makes many such verbs. From adjectives: ଗରମ → ଗରମେଇବା, ଚିକ୍କଣ → ଚିକ୍କଣେଇବା. From nouns: ଲାଜ → ଲାଜେଇବା, ଗୋଡ଼ → ଗୋଡ଼େଇବା ('to chase'). Why not the others: a ବିଶେଷ୍ୟ can also form a ନାମଧାତୁ, but ହୁଗୁଳା is a describing word (ହୁଗୁଳା ଦଉଡ଼ି, 'a loose rope'), not a naming word; ହୁଗୁଳା is not a pronoun (ସର୍ବନାମ) either; and ବଚନ (number: singular or plural) is a grammatical feature, not a class of words at all. Test: put the base word before a noun. If it describes the noun (ହୁଗୁଳା ଜାମା, ଗରମ ପାଣି), the verb comes from an adjective. Q147 tests the same idea: ନାମଧାତୁ verbs are built on naming and describing words, not on original verb roots.

Q 168 | odi | Grammar
______ ଏକ କ୍ରିୟା ବିଶେଷଣ ପଦ ।
(1) ଧୀର ପଦପାତ
(2) ଧୀର ପାଣି
(3) ଧୀରେ ଆସ
(4) ଧୀରେ ଧୀରେ ଅନ୍ଧକାର
= 3
~ In 'ଧୀରେ ଆସ' ('come slowly'), ଧୀରେ tells how the action ଆସ should happen, so it qualifies a verb and is a କ୍ରିୟାବିଶେଷଣ (adverb). Notice the form: the adjective ଧୀର ('slow, gentle') takes the ending ଏ to become the adverb ଧୀରେ, just as ସହଜ becomes ସହଜେ in 'ସେ ସହଜେ ଜିତିଲା'. In ଧୀର ପଦପାତ ('slow footsteps') and ଧୀର ପାଣି ('still water'), ଧୀର describes the nouns ପଦପାତ and ପାଣି, so it is an adjective (ବିଶେଷଣ). 'ଧୀରେ ଧୀରେ ଅନ୍ଧକାର' is only a phrase: there is no verb in it, so ଧୀରେ ଧୀରେ has nothing to qualify. It would need a verb, as in 'ଧୀରେ ଧୀରେ ଅନ୍ଧକାର ଘୋଟିଲା', to work as an adverb. Quick test (as in Q162): look at the word the modifier is attached to. A verb means adverb; a noun means adjective. Also remember the pattern in this question: ଧୀର (without ଏ) usually describes a noun, while ଧୀରେ (with ଏ) usually describes a verb. Examiners often set such pairs side by side to test exactly this difference.

Q 169 | odi | Grammar
ବାକ୍ୟରେ କ୍ରିୟାପଦ ସହିତ ଯେଉଁ ପଦର ଅନ୍ୱୟ ନଥାଏ ତାହାକୁ ______ କୁହାଯାଏ ।
(1) ସମ୍ବନ୍ଧ ପଦ
(2) ବିଧେୟ ପଦ
(3) ଉଦ୍ଦେଶ୍ୟ ପଦ
(4) ବିଭକ୍ତି ପ୍ରତ୍ୟୟ
= 1
~ A word in the genitive, the ସମ୍ବନ୍ଧ ପଦ (the sixth case, ଷଷ୍ଠୀ, marked by ର, ଙ୍କର, ମାନଙ୍କର), is linked to another noun, not to the verb. In 'ରାମର ଭାଇ ଆସିଲା', ରାମର tells whose brother; it connects with ଭାଇ, not with ଆସିଲା. Put any karaka question to the verb (କିଏ ଆସିଲା? — ଭାଇ) and ରାମର never comes up as an answer. That is why traditional grammar counts only six karakas, namely କର୍ତ୍ତା, କର୍ମ, କରଣ, ସମ୍ପ୍ରଦାନ, ଅପାଦାନ and ଅଧିକରଣ, and leaves out ସମ୍ବନ୍ଧ (and ସମ୍ବୋଧନ, the vocative). The other options are wrong: ଉଦ୍ଦେଶ୍ୟ ପଦ (the subject part) is tied directly to the verb, since the verb agrees with it in person and number; ବିଧେୟ ପଦ (the predicate) contains the verb itself; and ବିଭକ୍ତି ପ୍ରତ୍ୟୟ is a case ending, not a separate word of the sentence. Memory tip: କାରକ = relation with the verb (କ୍ରିୟା ସହ ଅନ୍ୱୟ); ସମ୍ବନ୍ଧ = relation between two nouns (ନାମ ସହ ନାମ). Another example: in 'ଗଛର ଫଳ ପାଚିଲା', ଗଛର goes with ଫଳ, not with ପାଚିଲା.

Q 170 | odi | Grammar
କୁଟିବା ଧାତୁର ମୂଳ ରୂପ ______ ଧାତୁ ।
(1) ତଦ୍ଭବ
(2) ତତ୍ସମ
(3) ଦେଶଜ
(4) ବୈଦେଶିକ
= 2
~ As per OPSC's key, the root of କୁଟିବା ('to pound, to beat') is a tatsama (ତତ୍ସମ) root: କୁଟ୍ is treated as a root taken from Sanskrit without any change of form, with only the Odia verb endings added (କୁଟ୍ + ଇବା = କୁଟିବା; କୁଟୁଛି, କୁଟିଲା). Other tatsama roots work the same way: ଗଣ୍ → ଗଣିବା, ଚଳ୍ → ଚଳିବା, ଭଜ୍ → ଭଜିବା, ରଚ୍ → ରଚିବା, ପୂଜ୍ → ପୂଜିବା. A tadbhava (ତଦ୍ଭବ) root is a Sanskrit root whose shape changed on its way through Prakrit: କୃ → କର୍ (କରିବା), ଶ୍ରୁ → ଶୁଣ୍ (ଶୁଣିବା), ଭୂ → ହେ (ହେବା, Q163). A ଦେଶଜ root is of native, non-Sanskrit origin, and a ବୈଦେଶିକ root comes from a foreign language, such as ବଦଳିବା from the Arabic badal ('change'). Test: compare the Odia root with its Sanskrit source. The same form means tatsama, a changed form means tadbhava, and no Sanskrit source at all means deshaja or foreign. Trap: tadbhava does not mean 'a different meaning'; it means a changed sound or shape of the same root, as ଶ୍ରୁ and ଶୁଣ୍ show.

Q 171 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
‘ମିଷ୍ଟଭାଷୀ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କର ପ୍ରିୟ’ – ଏହାର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟସ୍ତ ଜଟିଳ ବାକ୍ୟଟି ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର ।
(1) ଯେଉଁବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ମିଷ୍ଟଭାଷୀ, ସେ ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କର ପ୍ରିୟ ।
(2) ଯେଉଁବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ମିଷ୍ଟଭାଷୀ ହୁଏ, ସେ ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କର ପ୍ରିୟ ହୁଏ ।
(3) ମିଷ୍ଟଭାଷୀ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ହିଁ ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କର ପ୍ରିୟ ଅଟେ ।
(4) ସେ ମିଷ୍ଟଭାଷୀ ଓ ସେ ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କର ପ୍ରିୟ ।
= 1
~ A complex sentence (ଜଟିଳ ବାକ୍ୟ) has one principal clause and at least one dependent clause, often linked by a relative pair such as ଯେଉଁ … ସେ. Option (A), 'ଯେଉଁ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ମିଷ୍ଟଭାଷୀ, ସେ ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କର ପ୍ରିୟ', turns the adjective ମିଷ୍ଟଭାଷୀ ('sweet-spoken') into the dependent clause 'ଯେଉଁ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ମିଷ୍ଟଭାଷୀ' and keeps the principal clause 'ସେ ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କର ପ୍ରିୟ', with every original word and the same meaning. (B) uses the same pair but adds ହୁଏ to both clauses, which turns 'is dear' into 'becomes dear' and adds words not in the original, so OPSC's key prefers (A). (C) is still a simple sentence; ହିଁ and ଅଟେ only add emphasis. (D) is a compound sentence joined by ଓ, and it speaks of one particular person, losing the general truth. Rule for this set of questions: simple = one finite verb; compound = two independent clauses joined by ଓ, ଏବଂ, କିନ୍ତୁ or ମାତ୍ର; complex = a dependent clause opened by ଯେ, ଯେଉଁ, ଯେତେବେଳେ, ଯଦି or ଯେହେତୁ and answered by ସେ, ସେତେବେଳେ, ତେବେ or ତେଣୁ.

Q 172 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ଗୃହତ୍ୟାଗୀ ଗୌତମ ବୋଧଗୟାରେ ବୁଦ୍ଧତ୍ୱ ପ୍ରାପ୍ତ ହେଲେ – ଏହାର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟସ୍ତ ଜଟିଳ ବାକ୍ୟଟି ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର ।
(1) ଗୌତମ ଗୃହତ୍ୟାଗୀ ହେଲେ ଓ ବୋଧଗୟାରେ ବୁଦ୍ଧତ୍ୱ ପ୍ରାପ୍ତ ହେଲେ ।
(2) ଗୃହତ୍ୟାଗୀ ଗୌତମ ହିଁ ବୋଧଗୟାରେ ବୁଦ୍ଧତ୍ୱ ପ୍ରାପ୍ତ ହୋଇଥିଲେ ।
(3) ଯେଉଁ ଗୌତମ ଗୃହତ୍ୟାଗୀ ହୋଇଥିଲେ ସେ ବୋଧଗୟାରେ ବୁଦ୍ଧତ୍ୱ ପ୍ରାପ୍ତ ହେଲେ ।
(4) ଗୌତମ ଗୃହତ୍ୟାଗୀ ହେଲେ ବୋଲି ବୁଦ୍ଧତ୍ୱ ପ୍ରାପ୍ତ ହେଲେ ।
= 3
~ To make a complex sentence, the adjective ଗୃହତ୍ୟାଗୀ ('one who left home') is expanded into a dependent relative clause, 'ଯେଉଁ ଗୌତମ ଗୃହତ୍ୟାଗୀ ହୋଇଥିଲେ', and linked by ସେ to the principal clause 'ସେ ବୋଧଗୟାରେ ବୁଦ୍ଧତ୍ୱ ପ୍ରାପ୍ତ ହେଲେ'. The meaning stays the same, so (C) is right. Note the tense: ହୋଇଥିଲେ (past perfect) shows that leaving home came before enlightenment. (A) joins two independent clauses with ଓ, so it is compound, not complex. (B) is still simple; ହିଁ only adds emphasis. (D) does have two clauses, but ବୋଲି makes leaving home the reason for enlightenment, which the original never says. History link: Siddhartha Gautama left home at about 29 (the Great Renunciation) and attained enlightenment under the Bodhi tree at Bodh Gaya in Bihar; tradition holds that two merchants from Utkala, Tapassu and Bhallika, became his first lay followers. Tip: an adjective placed before a noun (ଗୃହତ୍ୟାଗୀ ଗୌତମ, ମିଷ୍ଟଭାଷୀ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି) is the easiest part to turn into a ଯେଉଁ … ସେ clause.

Q 173 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ଗଛ ଲଗାହେଲା ଓ ଚତୁର୍ଦ୍ଦିଗ ସବୁଜ ଶ୍ୟାମଳ ଦିଶିଲା – ଏହାର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟସ୍ତ ଜଟିଳ ବାକ୍ୟଟି ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର ।
(1) ଗଛ ଲଗାହେଲାରୁ ଚତୁର୍ଦ୍ଦିଗ ସବୁଜ ଶ୍ୟାମଳ ଦିଶିଲା ।
(2) ଗଛ ଲଗା ହେଉହେଉ ଚତୁର୍ଦ୍ଦିଗ ସବୁଜ ଶ୍ୟାମଳ ଦିଶିଲା ।
(3) ଗଛ ଲଗାହେଲା ଏବଂ ଚତୁର୍ଦ୍ଦିଗ ସବୁଜ ଶ୍ୟାମଳ ଦିଶିଲା ।
(4) ଯେତେବେଳେ ଗଛ ଲଗାହେଲା ସେତେବେଳେ ଚତୁର୍ଦ୍ଦିଗ ସବୁଜ ଶ୍ୟାମଳ ଦିଶିଲା ।
= 4
~ (D), 'ଯେତେବେଳେ ଗଛ ଲଗାହେଲା ସେତେବେଳେ ଚତୁର୍ଦ୍ଦିଗ ସବୁଜ ଶ୍ୟାମଳ ଦିଶିଲା', is the complex sentence: 'ଯେତେବେଳେ ଗଛ ଲଗାହେଲା' is a dependent time clause that cannot stand alone, and it is tied to the principal clause by the pair ଯେତେବେଳେ … ସେତେବେଳେ ('when … then'). The original was compound: two independent clauses joined by ଓ. (A) uses ଲଗାହେଲାରୁ ('since or after the trees were planted'), a non-finite form, so the sentence has only one finite verb (ଦିଶିଲା) and is simple. (B) uses ହେଉହେଉ ('as soon as'), also non-finite, so it too is simple. (C) only swaps ଓ for ଏବଂ, so it is still the same compound sentence. Other time pairs that make complex sentences: ଯେବେ … ତେବେ, ଯେତିକିବେଳେ … ସେତିକିବେଳେ. Remember the ଯ–ସ pattern: a ଯ-word opens the dependent clause and its ସ/ତ partner opens the main clause, as in ଯେଉଁ … ସେ, ଯେତେବେଳେ … ସେତେବେଳେ, ଯଦି … ତେବେ, ଯେହେତୁ … ତେଣୁ. Seeing such a pair is the quickest sign of a complex sentence.

Q 174 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ରାଜନୀତିରେ ଗୋଟିଏ ଦଳ ତ୍ୟାଗ କରି ଅନ୍ୟ ଦଳର ସଦସ୍ୟ ହୁଅନ୍ତି – ଏହାର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟସ୍ତ ଯୌଗିକ ବାକ୍ୟଟି ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର ।
(1) ରାଜନୀତିରେ ଗୋଟିଏ ଦଳ ତ୍ୟାଗ କରନ୍ତି ଏବଂ ଅନ୍ୟ ଦଳର ସଦସ୍ୟ ହୁଅନ୍ତି ।
(2) ଯିଏ ରାଜନୀତିରେ ଗୋଟିଏ ଦଳ ତ୍ୟାଗ କରେ ସେ ଅନ୍ୟ ଦଳରେ ସଦସ୍ୟ ହୁଏ ।
(3) ରାଜନୀତିରେ ଗୋଟିଏ ଦଳ ତ୍ୟାଗ କରିବାପରେ ଅନ୍ୟ ଦଳର ସଦସ୍ୟ ହୁଅନ୍ତି ।
(4) ରାଜନୀତିରେ ଗୋଟିଏ ଦଳ ତ୍ୟାଗ କରୁଥିବା ଲୋକ ଅନ୍ୟଦଳରେ ସଦସ୍ୟ ହୁଅନ୍ତି ।
= 1
~ A compound sentence (ଯୌଗିକ ବାକ୍ୟ) joins two independent clauses, each with its own finite verb, by a conjunction such as ଓ, ଏବଂ, କିନ୍ତୁ or ମାତ୍ର. The original is simple: ତ୍ୟାଗ କରି ('having left') is non-finite and ହୁଅନ୍ତି is the only finite verb. In (A) ତ୍ୟାଗ କରି becomes the finite ତ୍ୟାଗ କରନ୍ତି, and ଏବଂ links it to 'ଅନ୍ୟ ଦଳର ସଦସ୍ୟ ହୁଅନ୍ତି', so both actions now stand as equal clauses. (B) is complex because of the relative pair ଯିଏ … ସେ, and it also shifts to the singular. (C) is simple; କରିବାପରେ ('after leaving') is non-finite. (D) is simple too; ତ୍ୟାଗ କରୁଥିବା ଲୋକ ('a person who leaves') is a participle phrase describing the subject. Conversion rule, simple → compound: change each non-finite verb (-ଇ, -ବାପରେ, -ଲାରୁ forms) into a finite one of the same tense and person, then join the clauses with ଓ or ଏବଂ. Example: 'ସେ ଖାଇ ଶୋଇଲା' → 'ସେ ଖାଇଲା ଏବଂ ଶୋଇଲା'. Polity link: this kind of party switching by legislators is what the Anti-Defection Law in the Tenth Schedule (added by the 52nd Amendment, 1985) deals with.

Q 175 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ଯଦି ତୁମେ ନୀତିନିଷ୍ଠ ହୁଅ, ତେବେ ତୁମର ନୈତିକ ସାହସ ବୃଦ୍ଧି ପାଇବ – ଏହାର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟସ୍ତ ଯୌଗିକ ବାକ୍ୟଟି ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର ।
(1) ନୀତିନିଷ୍ଠ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତିମାନେ ନୈତିକ ଭାବରେ ସାହସୀ ହୋଇଥାନ୍ତି ।
(2) ତୁମେ ନୀତିନିଷ୍ଠ ହେଲେ ନୈତିକ ସାହସ ବୃଦ୍ଧିପାଇବ ।
(3) ତୁମେ ନୀତିନିଷ୍ଠ ହୁଅ ଏବଂ ତୁମର ନୈତିକ ସାହସ ବୃଦ୍ଧିପାଇବ ।
(4) ଯେଉଁ ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ନୀତିନିଷ୍ଠ ହୁଏ, ତାହାର ନୈତିକ ସାହସ ବୃଦ୍ଧିପାଏ ।
= 3
~ The original 'ଯଦି ତୁମେ ନୀତିନିଷ୍ଠ ହୁଅ, ତେବେ ତୁମର ନୈତିକ ସାହସ ବୃଦ୍ଧି ପାଇବ' is complex: ଯଦି … ତେବେ makes the first clause a condition. To make it compound, turn the condition into an independent clause, here a piece of advice ('ତୁମେ ନୀତିନିଷ୍ଠ ହୁଅ'), and join the result with ଏବଂ: 'ତୁମେ ନୀତିନିଷ୍ଠ ହୁଅ ଏବଂ ତୁମର ନୈତିକ ସାହସ ବୃଦ୍ଧିପାଇବ'. English does the same: 'If you work hard, you will succeed' → 'Work hard and you will succeed'. So (C) is correct. (A) is simple and changes the meaning into a general statement about principled people. (B) is simple: ହେଲେ ('if you are') is a non-finite conditional form, leaving one finite verb, ବୃଦ୍ଧିପାଇବ. (D) is complex, built on ଯେଉଁ … ତାହାର, and it changes 'you' into 'whoever'. Conditional conversions to remember: complex 'ଯଦି … ତେବେ' ↔ simple '-ଲେ' (ପଢ଼ିଲେ ପାସ୍ କରିବ) ↔ compound 'command + ଏବଂ' (ପଢ଼ ଏବଂ ପାସ୍ କରିବ). Trap: don't pick (B) just because it keeps the meaning; it has only one finite verb, so it is simple.

Q 176 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ଜହ୍ନରେ କଳଙ୍କ ଥିବାରୁ ଏହା ସୁନ୍ଦର ଦିଶେ – ଏହାର ରୂପାନ୍ତରିତ ଯୌଗିକ ବାକ୍ୟଟି ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର ।
(1) କଳଙ୍କ ଜହ୍ନକୁ ସୁନ୍ଦର କରିଛି ।
(2) ସୁନ୍ଦର ଦିଶିବା ନିମନ୍ତେ ଜହ୍ନରେ କଳଙ୍କ ଅଛି ।
(3) ଯେଉଁ ଜହ୍ନରେ କଳଙ୍କ ଅଛି, ସେଇ ସୁନ୍ଦର ଦିଶେ ।
(4) ଜହ୍ନରେ କଳଙ୍କ ଅଛି ଓ ଏହା ସୁନ୍ଦର ଦିଶେ ।
= 4
~ The original 'ଜହ୍ନରେ କଳଙ୍କ ଥିବାରୁ ଏହା ସୁନ୍ଦର ଦିଶେ' is simple: ଥିବାରୁ ('because there is') is non-finite and ଦିଶେ is the only finite verb. In (D) ଥିବାରୁ becomes the finite ଅଛି, giving two independent clauses, 'ଜହ୍ନରେ କଳଙ୍କ ଅଛି' and 'ଏହା ସୁନ୍ଦର ଦିଶେ', joined by ଓ, so it is a compound sentence. (A) 'କଳଙ୍କ ଜହ୍ନକୁ ସୁନ୍ଦର କରିଛି' is simple, with one finite verb. (B) is simple and turns cause into purpose ('ସୁନ୍ଦର ଦିଶିବା ନିମନ୍ତେ', 'in order to look beautiful'). (C) is complex, with the relative pair ଯେଉଁ … ସେଇ, and it also changes the sense to 'only the moon that has a spot looks beautiful'. Note: a compound sentence can also keep the cause with ତେଣୁ or ସେଥିପାଇଁ ('ଜହ୍ନରେ କଳଙ୍କ ଅଛି, ତେଣୁ ଏହା ସୁନ୍ଦର ଦିଶେ'), but among the given options only (D) is compound. Spotting tip: count the finite verbs and look at the joining word. Two finite verbs + ଓ/ଏବଂ/କିନ୍ତୁ/ତେଣୁ = compound; two finite verbs + a ଯ-word pair = complex; one finite verb = simple.

Q 177 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ଯେଉଁ ଛାତ୍ର କଠୋର ପରିଶ୍ରମ କରେ, ସେ ପରୀକ୍ଷାରେ କୃତକାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ହୋଇଥାଏ – ଏହାର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟସ୍ତ ସରଳ ବାକ୍ୟଟି ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର ।
(1) ଯଦି ଛାତ୍ର କଠୋର ପରିଶ୍ରମ କରିବ, ତେବେ ପରୀକ୍ଷାରେ କୃତକାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ହେବ ।
(2) ଛାତ୍ର କଠୋର ପରିଶ୍ରମ କର, ପରୀକ୍ଷାରେ କୃତକାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ହେବ ।
(3) କଠୋର ପରିଶ୍ରମ କରୁଥିବା ଛାତ୍ର ହିଁ ପରୀକ୍ଷାରେ କୃତକାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ହୋଇଥାଏ ।
(4) ଛାତ୍ରଟି କଠୋର ପରିଶ୍ରମ କରିଲାରୁ ପରୀକ୍ଷାରେ କୃତକାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ହେଲା ।
= 3
~ The original 'ଯେଉଁ ଛାତ୍ର କଠୋର ପରିଶ୍ରମ କରେ, ସେ ପରୀକ୍ଷାରେ କୃତକାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ହୋଇଥାଏ' is complex (ଯେଉଁ … ସେ). A simple sentence has only one finite verb, so the dependent clause must shrink into a phrase. In (C) 'ଯେଉଁ ଛାତ୍ର କଠୋର ପରିଶ୍ରମ କରେ' becomes the participle phrase 'କଠୋର ପରିଶ୍ରମ କରୁଥିବା ଛାତ୍ର' ('the student who works hard'), leaving one finite verb, ହୋଇଥାଏ. The general, habitual meaning stays, and ହିଁ simply stresses it. (A) is still complex, now with ଯଦି … ତେବେ, and it shifts to the future. (B) has two finite verbs, the command କର and ହେବ, and turns a statement into advice to a student. (D) is simple in form, but ଛାତ୍ରଟି ('that particular student') and the past tense ହେଲା change a general truth into one event. Rule for complex → simple: replace the relative clause with a -ଉଥିବା or -ଇଥିବା participle placed before the noun, e.g. 'ଯେଉଁ ପିଲା ଗୀତ ଗାଉଛି' → 'ଗୀତ ଗାଉଥିବା ପିଲା'. Keep the tense and the general or particular sense of the original.

Q 178 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ଯେହେତୁ ତୁମ ଦେହ ଖରାପ ଅଛି, ତେଣୁ ତୁମେ ବୁଲାବୁଲି କରନାହିଁ – ଏହାର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟସ୍ତ ସରଳ ବାକ୍ୟଟି ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର।
(1) ତୁମ ଦେହ ଖରାପ ଅଛି, ତୁମେ ବୁଲାବୁଲି କରନାହିଁ।
(2) ଦେହ ଖରାପ ଥିଲେ, ବୁଲାବୁଲି କରନ୍ତି ନାହିଁ ।
(3) ତୁମ ଦେହ ଖରାପ ଥିବାରୁ ବୁଲାବୁଲି କରନାହିଁ ।
(4) ତୁମ ଦେହ ଖରାପ ଥିବା ସତ୍ତ୍ୱେ ବୁଲାବୁଲି କରୁଛ।
= 3
~ The original 'ଯେହେତୁ ତୁମ ଦେହ ଖରାପ ଅଛି, ତେଣୁ ତୁମେ ବୁଲାବୁଲି କରନାହିଁ' is complex: ଯେହେତୁ … ତେଣୁ ('since … therefore') joins a reason clause to a piece of advice. In (C) the reason clause becomes the non-finite ଥିବାରୁ ('because … is'): 'ତୁମ ଦେହ ଖରାପ ଥିବାରୁ ବୁଲାବୁଲି କରନାହିଁ'. Only one finite verb remains (କରନାହିଁ, 'don't roam about'), and the meaning is unchanged, so it is a simple sentence. (A) just drops the connectives but still has two finite verbs, ଅଛି and କରନାହିଁ, so it is not simple. (B) turns cause into condition (ଥିଲେ = 'if'), drops ତୁମ, and changes the advice into a general statement (କରନ୍ତି ନାହିଁ, 'people don't'). (D) reverses the sense: ସତ୍ତ୍ୱେ means 'in spite of', and କରୁଛ says you are roaming about. Cause markers to remember for simple sentences: -ବାରୁ, -ଥିବାରୁ, ଯୋଗୁଁ, ପାଇଁ, as in 'ବର୍ଷା ହେବାରୁ ସ୍କୁଲ ବନ୍ଦ ରହିଲା'. Trap: removing ଯେହେତୁ and ତେଣୁ alone (option A) does not make a sentence simple; always count the finite verbs.

Q 179 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ରାମବାବୁ ସବୁ ଶୁଣିଲେ ଏବଂ କିଛି କହିଲେ ନାହିଁ – ଏହାର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟସ୍ତ ସରଳ ବାକ୍ୟଟି ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର ।
(1) ରାମବାବୁ ଯେତେବେଳେ ସବୁ ଶୁଣିଲେ ସେତେବେଳେ କିଛି କହିଲେ ନାହିଁ ।
(2) ରାମବାବୁ ସବୁ ଶୁଣିଲେ ହେଁ କିଛି କହିଲେ ନାହିଁ।
(3) ରାମବାବୁ ସବୁ ଶୁଣିଲେ ନାହିଁ କି କିଛି କହିଲେ ନାହିଁ ।
(4) ରାମବାବୁ ସବୁ ଶୁଣିଲେ, ମାତ୍ର କିଛି କହିଲେ ନାହିଁ।
= 2
~ The original 'ରାମବାବୁ ସବୁ ଶୁଣିଲେ ଏବଂ କିଛି କହିଲେ ନାହିଁ' is compound: two finite verbs (ଶୁଣିଲେ, କହିଲେ ନାହିଁ) joined by ଏବଂ. In (B) 'ଶୁଣିଲେ ହେଁ' means 'even though he heard'; here ଶୁଣିଲେ is the non-finite conditional form and ହେଁ adds 'even'. So the only finite verb is କହିଲେ ନାହିଁ, and the sentence is simple, keeping the sense of hearing everything yet saying nothing. (A) is complex, with the time pair ଯେତେବେଳେ … ସେତେବେଳେ. (D) is still compound, now joined by ମାତ୍ର ('but'). (C) changes the meaning to 'he neither heard nor spoke'. Trap: the same form ଶୁଣିଲେ can be finite ('he heard', the honorific past, as in A and D) or non-finite ('if or when he hears', as in B), so look at what follows. If ହେଁ or ମଧ୍ୟ follows, it is non-finite. More examples of this simple-sentence pattern: 'ସେ ଡାକିଲେ ହେଁ ମୁଁ ଗଲି ନାହିଁ' ('though he called, I did not go'), 'ବହୁତ ଖୋଜିଲେ ମଧ୍ୟ ମିଳିଲା ନାହିଁ' ('even after much searching, it was not found').

Q 180 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ସୀତା କହିଲେ, “ହନୁମାନ ଏ ମୁଦ୍ରିକା ଗ୍ରହଣ କର” – କେଉଁ ପ୍ରକାରର ବାକ୍ୟ ?
(1) ପରୋକ୍ଷ ଉକ୍ତି ମୂଳକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(2) ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷ ଉକ୍ତି ମୂଳକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(3) ବିସ୍ମୟାତ୍ମକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(4) ପ୍ରଶ୍ନାତ୍ମକ ବାକ୍ୟ
= 2
~ The sentence gives Sita's exact words inside quotation marks after the reporting verb କହିଲେ, so it is a direct-speech sentence (ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷ ଉକ୍ତି ମୂଳକ ବାକ୍ୟ). Its signs are a reporting verb (କହିଲେ, ପଚାରିଲେ), a comma, and the speaker's words unchanged inside quotes; even the address 'ହନୁମାନ' and the word ଏ ('this') stay as spoken. In indirect speech (ପରୋକ୍ଷ ଉକ୍ତି) the quotes go and the words are reported: 'ସୀତା ହନୁମାନଙ୍କୁ ସେହି ମୁଦ୍ରିକା ଗ୍ରହଣ କରିବାକୁ କହିଲେ'. Notice the changes: ଏ → ସେହି, the command ଗ୍ରହଣ କର → ଗ୍ରହଣ କରିବାକୁ, and the vocative ହନୁମାନ → ହନୁମାନଙ୍କୁ. It is not a ବିସ୍ମୟାତ୍ମକ (exclamatory) sentence, since there is no ଆହା!, ବାଃ! or exclamation mark, and not a ପ୍ରଶ୍ନାତ୍ମକ (interrogative) sentence, since nothing is asked. The quoted words themselves form an ଆଜ୍ଞାସୂଚକ (imperative) sentence, which is why the indirect form uses '-ବାକୁ କହିଲେ' (or ଆଦେଶ ଦେଲେ / ଅନୁରୋଧ କଲେ). Tip: quotation marks = ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷ; no quotation marks and a changed pronoun = ପରୋକ୍ଷ.

Q 181 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ଅଶୋକ ନିଜର ସମସ୍ତ ପ୍ରଜା ସୁଖରେ ରହନ୍ତୁ ବୋଲି ପ୍ରାର୍ଥନା କଲେ – କେଉଁ ପ୍ରକାରର ବାକ୍ୟ ?
(1) କାମନାତ୍ମକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(2) ବିସ୍ମୟାତ୍ମକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(3) ବିବରଣାତ୍ମକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(4) ଆଦେଶାତ୍ମକ ବାକ୍ୟ
= 1
~ A କାମନାତ୍ମକ ବାକ୍ୟ (optative sentence) expresses a wish, blessing or prayer, and that is exactly what Ashoka's words carry: he prayed that all his subjects may live happily. The tell-tale signs are the wish form of the verb ରହନ୍ତୁ ('may they live') and the verb ପ୍ରାର୍ଥନା କଲେ ('prayed'). In direct form it reads: ଅଶୋକ ପ୍ରାର୍ଥନା କଲେ, “ମୋର ସମସ୍ତ ପ୍ରଜା ସୁଖରେ ରହନ୍ତୁ” — a clear wish. The other choices do not fit: a ବିସ୍ମୟାତ୍ମକ (exclamatory) sentence shows sudden wonder, joy or grief, usually with words like ଆହା, ବାଃ or କି ସୁନ୍ଦର; a ବିବରଣାତ୍ମକ (assertive) sentence only states a fact, such as 'ଅଶୋକ କଳିଙ୍ଗ ଜୟ କଲେ'; an ଆଦେଶାତ୍ମକ (imperative) sentence gives an order or request, such as 'ଏଠାକୁ ଆସ'. Trap: because the wish is reported with ବୋଲି, the sentence may look like a plain statement, but here it is classed by its content, which is a prayer. More optative examples: 'ଭଗବାନ ତୁମର ମଙ୍ଗଳ କରନ୍ତୁ', 'ତୁମେ ଦୀର୍ଘଜୀବୀ ହୁଅ'. Odisha link: in his separate Kalinga edicts at Dhauli and Jaugada, Ashoka says 'all men are my children' and wishes them welfare in this world and the next.

Q 182 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ଆଦେଶାତ୍ମକ ବାକ୍ୟଟି ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର ।
(1) ତୁମେ କାହା କଥା ଶୁଣିଲେ ଚଳିବ କି ?
(2) ତୁମେ କାହା କଥା ଶୁଣିଲେ ଚଳିବ ନାହିଁ ବୋଲି ରାମ କହିଲା ।
(3) ତୁମେ କାହାକଥା ଶୁଣିବା ଦରକାର ନାହିଁ ।
(4) କେଉଁଟି ନୁହଁ ।
= 3
~ 'ତୁମେ କାହାକଥା ଶୁଣିବା ଦରକାର ନାହିଁ' ('You need not listen to anyone') speaks straight to the listener (ତୁମେ) and tells him what not to do, so OPSC's key treats it as the imperative (ଆଦେଶାତ୍ମକ) sentence. An imperative sentence gives an order, instruction, advice or prohibition; its subject is the second person, often left unsaid, as in 'କାହା କଥା ଶୁଣ ନାହିଁ' or 'ଚୁପ୍ ରୁହ'. The other choices: (A) ends with କି and a question mark, so it is interrogative (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନସୂଚକ) — in fact a rhetorical question carrying a similar idea; (B) is indirect speech (ପରୋକ୍ଷ ଉକ୍ତି): it only reports, through ବୋଲି, what Ram said, so it is a statement; (D) fails because (C) fits. Questions 171–190 test sentence transformation: one idea can be put as a question (A), a reported statement (B) or a direct instruction to the listener (C). Tip: ask whom the sentence addresses and whether it pushes the listener to do or not do something — that is the mark of an ଆଦେଶାତ୍ମକ sentence.

Q 183 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ସେନାପତି କହିଲେ, “କେତେବେଳେ ଶତ୍ରୁମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିପାତ କରିବା ?” – ଏହାର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟସ୍ତ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନାତ୍ମକ ବାକ୍ୟଟି ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର ।
(1) ସେନାପତି ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ପଚାରିଲେ ଯେ ଆମେ କେତେବେଳେ ଶତ୍ରୁମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିପାତ କରିବା ?
(2) ସେନାପତି କହିଲେ ସେମାନେ କେତେବେଳେ ଶତ୍ରୁମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିପାତ କରିବେ ?
(3) ସେନାପତି ପଚାରିଲେ ଯେ କେତେବେଳେ ଶତ୍ରୁମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିପାତ କରାଯିବ ।
(4) କେଉଁଟି ନୁହଁ ।
= 3
~ 'ସେନାପତି ପଚାରିଲେ ଯେ କେତେବେଳେ ଶତ୍ରୁମାନଙ୍କୁ ନିପାତ କରାଯିବ ।' is the correct indirect (ପରୋକ୍ଷ) form of the commander's question. The rules for turning a direct question into indirect speech in Odia: the reporting verb କହିଲେ becomes ପଚାରିଲେ; the quotation marks go and the conjunction ଯେ joins the two parts (for a yes/no question, କି ନାହିଁ is added, e.g. … ପଚାରିଲେ ଯେ ସେ ଯିବ କି ନାହିଁ); the first person in the quoted words (here the hidden ଆମେ in କରିବା) must change, so the clause becomes 'ସେମାନେ … କରିବେ' or, as here, the passive 'କରାଯିବ'; and the question mark gives way to a full stop (।), because the reported sentence is now a statement. (A) keeps ଆମେ … କରିବା and the question mark, and 'ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ପଚାରିଲେ' is redundant. (B) changes the person correctly but keeps କହିଲେ, has no ଯେ and still ends with '?'. (D) fails because (C) is right. Memory tip for a reported question: ପଚାରିଲେ + ଯେ + changed person + ।

Q 184 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
‘ଆଜି ମୋର ଡେରିରେ ଖାଇବା ହେଲା’ – ବାକ୍ୟଟି ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର ।
(1) କର୍ମବାଚ୍ୟ ମୂଳକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(2) ଭାବବାଚ୍ୟ ମୂଳକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(3) କର୍ତ୍ତୃବାଚ୍ୟ ମୂଳକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(4) ବିବୃତ୍ତିସୂଚକ ବାଚ୍ୟ
= 2
~ 'ଆଜି ମୋର ଡେରିରେ ଖାଇବା ହେଲା' is in the impersonal voice (ଭାବବାଚ୍ୟ), where the action itself (ଭାବ) is the focus, not the doer or an object. Its marks: the doer appears in the possessive form (ମୋର, not ମୁଁ); no object is named; and the main verb becomes a verbal noun (ଖାଇବା) followed by ହେବା (here ହେଲା), which stays in the third person whoever the doer is. In the active voice (କର୍ତ୍ତୃବାଚ୍ୟ) the same idea is 'ମୁଁ ଆଜି ଡେରିରେ ଖାଇଲି', where the verb agrees with the doer ମୁଁ. The passive voice (କର୍ମବାଚ୍ୟ) needs an object brought to the front, e.g. 'ମୋ ଦ୍ୱାରା ଭାତ ଖିଆଗଲା'; there is no object here. 'ବିବୃତିସୂଚକ' names a type of sentence by meaning (a statement), not a voice at all, so (D) is only a distractor. Odia grammar has three voices: କର୍ତ୍ତୃବାଚ୍ୟ, କର୍ମବାଚ୍ୟ and ଭାବବାଚ୍ୟ. More ଭାବବାଚ୍ୟ examples: 'ମୋର ରାତିରେ ଶୋଇବା ହେଲା ନାହିଁ', 'ତାଙ୍କ ଦ୍ୱାରା ଆଉ ଚାଲି ହେଉନାହିଁ'. Tip: doer in possessive or with ଦ୍ୱାରା + verbal noun + ହେବା/ଯିବା, and no object = ଭାବବାଚ୍ୟ.

Q 185 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ଭାରତର ପ୍ରଧାନମନ୍ତ୍ରୀ ଇନ୍ଦିରା ଗାନ୍ଧି ତାଙ୍କ ଦେହରକ୍ଷୀଙ୍କ ଦ୍ୱାରା ନିହତ ହୋଇଥିଲେ – ଏହା କେଉଁ ପ୍ରକାରର ବାକ୍ୟ ?
(1) ଜଟିଳ ବାକ୍ୟ
(2) ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷୋକ୍ତି ମୂଳକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(3) ସରଳ ବାକ୍ୟ
(4) କେଉଁଟି ନୁହଁ
= 3
~ By structure, 'ଭାରତର ପ୍ରଧାନମନ୍ତ୍ରୀ ଇନ୍ଦିରା ଗାନ୍ଧି ତାଙ୍କ ଦେହରକ୍ଷୀଙ୍କ ଦ୍ୱାରା ନିହତ ହୋଇଥିଲେ' is a simple sentence (ସରଳ ବାକ୍ୟ): it has one subject (ଇନ୍ଦିରା ଗାନ୍ଧି, with ଭାରତର ପ୍ରଧାନମନ୍ତ୍ରୀ only describing her) and just one finite verb (ନିହତ ହୋଇଥିଲେ). A complex sentence (ଜଟିଳ ବାକ୍ୟ) needs a main clause plus a dependent clause joined by words like ଯେ, ଯଦି, ଯେହେତୁ or ଯେଉଁ … ସେହି, e.g. 'ଯେଉଁ ଦେହରକ୍ଷୀମାନେ ତାଙ୍କୁ ଜଗିଥିଲେ, ସେମାନେ ହିଁ ତାଙ୍କୁ ହତ୍ୟା କଲେ'. A direct-speech sentence (ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷୋକ୍ତି) quotes someone's exact words inside quotation marks, which this sentence does not do. So (D) is out as well. Don't be misled by ଦ୍ୱାରା: it shows the sentence is in the passive voice (କର୍ମବାଚ୍ୟ), which is about voice, not structure; the active form 'ତାଙ୍କ ଦେହରକ୍ଷୀମାନେ ଇନ୍ଦିରା ଗାନ୍ଧିଙ୍କୁ ହତ୍ୟା କରିଥିଲେ' is also simple. Background: Indira Gandhi was shot dead by two of her bodyguards in New Delhi on 31 October 1984; the day before, she had addressed a public meeting in Bhubaneswar. Remember the three structural types: ସରଳ (one finite verb), ଜଟିଳ (main + dependent clause), ଯୌଗିକ (two independent clauses joined by ଓ, ଏବଂ, କିନ୍ତୁ, ତେଣୁ).

Q 186 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ମଧୁବାବୁ ପୁଣି ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ କାହାର ଅଜଣା – ବାକ୍ୟଟି ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର ।
(1) ବିସ୍ମୟସୂଚକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(2) ପ୍ରଶ୍ନସୂଚକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(3) ବିବୃତ୍ତିସୂଚକ
(4) ପରୋକ୍ଷ ଉକ୍ତି ମୂଳକ ବାକ୍ୟ
= 1
~ 'ମଧୁବାବୁ ପୁଣି ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ କାହାର ଅଜଣା' ('Madhubabu — who in Odisha does not know him!') is treated by OPSC as an exclamatory sentence (ବିସ୍ମୟସୂଚକ ବାକ୍ୟ). The speaker is not seeking information; he expresses wonder and pride that anyone in Odisha could be unaware of Madhubabu, and the emphatic word ପୁଣି carries that feeling. Its plain meaning is 'ଓଡ଼ିଶାରେ ସମସ୍ତେ ମଧୁବାବୁଙ୍କୁ ଜାଣନ୍ତି', which would be the assertive (ବିବୃତିସୂଚକ) transformation; the given sentence is not in that plain form, so (C) is wrong. (B) fails because no answer is expected, and the sentence as printed has no question mark. (D) fails because nobody's words are being reported with ଯେ or ବୋଲି. Trap: emotional lines that look like questions are tested often; judge them by the feeling they express. Context: Madhubabu is Madhusudan Das (1848–1934), honoured as 'Utkal Gourab', who founded the Utkal Sammilani in 1903 to unite the Odia-speaking areas — which is why every Odia knows him. A similar sentence: 'ଜଗନ୍ନାଥଙ୍କୁ ପୁଣି କିଏ ନ ଜାଣେ!'

Q 187 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
______ ଭାବବାଚ୍ୟ ମୂଳକ ବାକ୍ୟ ?
(1) ଆମ ବିଦ୍ୟାଳୟରେ ଭଲ ପଢ଼ା ହୁଏ ।
(2) ମୋତେ କିଛି ଜଣାନାହିଁ ।
(3) ଘଣ୍ଟି ବାଜୁଛି ।
(4) ଏ ପୃଥିବୀରୁ ଦୁଃଖ ଯିବ କିପରି ?
= 1
~ 'ଆମ ବିଦ୍ୟାଳୟରେ ଭଲ ପଢ଼ା ହୁଏ' ('Studies go on well in our school') is the impersonal-voice (ଭାବବାଚ୍ୟ) sentence. No doer (who teaches or studies) and no object (what is studied) is named; the verb takes the form ପଢ଼ା + ହୁଏ, so only the action — studying — is in focus. That is the sign of ଭାବବାଚ୍ୟ. (B) 'ମୋତେ କିଛି ଜଣାନାହିଁ' simply means 'ମୁଁ କିଛି ଜାଣେ ନାହିଁ'; it has a subject, କିଛି ('nothing is known to me'), so it is not a bare-action sentence. (C) 'ଘଣ୍ଟି ବାଜୁଛି' is plain active voice (କର୍ତ୍ତୃବାଚ୍ୟ): the subject ଘଣ୍ଟି does the action ବାଜୁଛି. (D) 'ଏ ପୃଥିବୀରୁ ଦୁଃଖ ଯିବ କିପରି?' is an active-voice question with ଦୁଃଖ as the subject of ଯିବ. Compare the three voices with one verb: 'ରାମ ବହି ପଢ଼େ' (କର୍ତ୍ତୃବାଚ୍ୟ), 'ରାମ ଦ୍ୱାରା ବହି ପଢ଼ାଯାଏ' (କର୍ମବାଚ୍ୟ), 'ଏଠାରେ ଭଲ ପଢ଼ା ହୁଏ' (ଭାବବାଚ୍ୟ). More examples of ଭାବବାଚ୍ୟ: 'ଏଠାରେ ବସାଯାଉ', 'ଆଜି ଆଉ ଯିବା ହେବ ନାହିଁ'. Tip: no doer, no object, and the verb in its ଆ/ବା form + ହେବା/ଯିବା → ଭାବବାଚ୍ୟ.

Q 188 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷ ଉକ୍ତିରେ କର୍ତ୍ତା ______ ହୋଇଥିଲେ ପରୋକ୍ଷ ଉକ୍ତିରେ ତାହା ତୃତୀୟ ପୁରୁଷ ହୁଏ ।
(1) ତୃତୀୟ ପୁରୁଷ
(2) ଦ୍ୱିତୀୟ ପୁରୁଷ
(3) ପ୍ରଥମ ପୁରୁଷ
(4) କର୍ମ
= 3
~ ପ୍ରଥମ ପୁରୁଷ (first person, the speaker: ମୁଁ, ଆମେ) is the person that becomes ତୃତୀୟ ପୁରୁଷ (third person: ସେ, ସେମାନେ) when direct speech is turned into indirect speech, because the reporter now talks about the speaker. Example: ରାମ କହିଲା, “ମୁଁ କାଲି ଯିବି” → ରାମ କହିଲା ଯେ ସେ କାଲି ଯିବ; ମୁଁ → ସେ, and the verb ଯିବି → ଯିବ follows. Why not the others: a third-person subject (A) is already third person and stays unchanged, so it does not 'become' third person; a second-person subject (B: ତୁ, ତୁମେ, ଆପଣ) changes according to the listener — it turns third person only when the listener is a third person, e.g. ରାମ ଶ୍ୟାମକୁ କହିଲା, “ତୁମେ ଭଲ ପିଲା” → ରାମ ଶ୍ୟାମକୁ କହିଲା ଯେ ସେ ଭଲ ପିଲା; and କର୍ମ (D, the object) is a case relation, not a person. Exception: if the reporter is the speaker himself (ମୁଁ କହିଲି, “ମୁଁ ଯିବି”), the first person stays: ମୁଁ କହିଲି ଯେ ମୁଁ ଯିବି. Trap: this question numbers persons in the English order, so ପ୍ରଥମ = speaker; don't mix it up with Sanskrit, where prathama purusha means the third person and the speaker is uttama purusha.

Q 189 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
‘ଯେ’ କେଉଁ ବାକ୍ୟରେ ବ୍ୟବହାର ହୁଏ ?
(1) ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷ ଉକ୍ତି
(2) ପରୋକ୍ଷ ଉକ୍ତି
(3) କର୍ମବାଚ୍ୟ
(4) ପ୍ରଶ୍ନସୂଚକ ବାକ୍ୟ
= 2
~ The conjunction ଯେ ('that') is the mark of indirect speech (ପରୋକ୍ଷ ଉକ୍ତି). When a speaker's exact words are reported in our own words, the comma and quotation marks of direct speech are dropped and ଯେ joins the reporting verb to the reported words: ସେ କହିଲା, “ମୁଁ ଯିବି” → ସେ କହିଲା ଯେ ସେ ଯିବ. Odia can also do this with ବୋଲି placed after the reported words: ସେ ଯିବ ବୋଲି କହିଲା. Why the others are wrong: direct speech (ପ୍ରତ୍ୟକ୍ଷ ଉକ୍ତି) keeps the speaker's words unchanged inside quotation marks and does not use ଯେ; the passive voice (କର୍ମବାଚ୍ୟ) is marked by ଦ୍ୱାରା and verb forms like ଲେଖାଗଲା or କରାଯାଏ, as in 'ରାମ ଦ୍ୱାରା ଚିଠି ଲେଖାଗଲା'; an interrogative sentence (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନସୂଚକ) uses କି, କିଏ, କ'ଣ or କେବେ and ends with '?'. Note that ଯେ also joins clauses in complex sentences ('ମୁଁ ଜାଣେ ଯେ ସେ ସତ କହୁଛି'), but among these options it points to indirect speech. Memory tip: direct = “ ” (quotation marks); indirect = ଯେ … or … ବୋଲି.

Q 190 | odi | Grammar
ନିମ୍ନ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନଗୁଡ଼ିକରେ ବାକ୍ୟର ପୁର୍ନବିନ୍ୟାସ ବା ରୂପାନ୍ତରଣ ଘଟିଛି । ଏଥିମଧ୍ୟରୁ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ ଅନୁସାରେ ଠିକ୍ ବାକ୍ୟ ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର । (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନ 171 ରୁ 190)
ଫୁଲଟି କେଡେ ସୁନ୍ଦର ବୋଲି ନିର୍ମଳ କହିଲା – ଏ ବାକ୍ୟଟି ଚିହ୍ନଟ କର ।
(1) ବିସ୍ମୟ ସୂଚକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(2) ପ୍ରଶ୍ନସୂଚକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(3) ବିବୃତି ସୂଚକ ବାକ୍ୟ
(4) ଆଦେଶସୂଚକ ବାକ୍ୟ
= 1
~ OPSC's key classes 'ଫୁଲଟି କେଡେ ସୁନ୍ଦର ବୋଲି ନିର୍ମଳ କହିଲା' as an exclamatory sentence (ବିସ୍ମୟ ସୂଚକ ବାକ୍ୟ), because what Nirmal said is an outburst of wonder and delight. The word କେଡେ ('how very') is an exclamatory word, like କି or କେତେ in 'କି ସୁନ୍ଦର!'. In direct speech the line is: ନିର୍ମଳ କହିଲା, “ଆହା! ଫୁଲଟି କେଡେ ସୁନ୍ଦର!”; reported with ବୋଲି, the feeling is still kept by କେଡେ. Why not the others: it asks nothing, so it is not ପ୍ରଶ୍ନସୂଚକ (B); it gives no order or request, so it is not ଆଦେଶସୂଚକ (D); and though the frame '… ବୋଲି ନିର୍ମଳ କହିଲା' looks like a plain statement (ବିବୃତି, C), the key goes by the emotion in the reported words. Trap: in this paper, a reported sentence that keeps an exclamatory word such as କେଡେ is judged by its feeling. To make it a pure statement you would write 'ନିର୍ମଳ କହିଲା ଯେ ଫୁଲଟି ବହୁତ ସୁନ୍ଦର'. Another exclamatory example: 'ବାଃ! କି ସୁନ୍ଦର ଦୃଶ୍ୟ!'

Q 191 | odi | Literature & Comprehension
ନିର୍ଦ୍ଦେଶ (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନସଂଖ୍ୟା 191 ରୁ 195) : ଅନୁଚ୍ଛେଦଟିକୁ ସାବଧାନତା ସହକାରେ ପଢ଼ି ନିମ୍ନୋକ୍ତ ପ୍ରଶ୍ନର ଉତ୍ତର ଦିଅ ।
୧୯୩୦ ମସିହା ଅକ୍ଟୋବର ୧୨ ତାରିଖ, ମାନବ ଜାତିର ଇତିହାସରେ ଗୋଟିଏ ପ୍ରଧାନ ଦିବସ । ଅଦ୍ୟ ଭାରତରେ ଏପରି ଗୋଟିଏ ମହତ୍ କର୍ମାନୁଷ୍ଠାନ କରାଗଲା ଯାହା ବଳରେ ଭାରତର ସୁପ୍ତ ଶକ୍ତି ଜାଗି ଉଠିଲା ଏବଂ ଭାରତର ଲୁପ୍ତ ଗୌରବର ରଶ୍ମିରେଖା ପ୍ରଚଣ୍ଡ ତେଜରେ ପ୍ରତିଭାତ ହୋଇ କୋଟି କୋଟି ଭାରତବାସୀଙ୍କ ହୃଦୟରୁ ପରାଧୀନତାର କାଳିମାକୁ ଚିରକାଳ ନିମିତ୍ତ ଅପସାରିତ କରିବାକୁ ଏକ ନୂତନ ଆଲୋକ ପ୍ରଦାନ କରିଥିଲା । ଅଦ୍ୟ ମହାତ୍ମାଗାନ୍ଧୀ ‘ମନ୍ତ୍ରଂ ବା ସାଧୟେତ୍ ଶରୀରଂ ବା ପାତୟେତ୍’ ମନ୍ତ୍ରରେ ଭାରତବାସୀଙ୍କୁ ଦୀକ୍ଷିତ କରି ସାବରତୀ ଆଶ୍ରମରୁ ସମର ଯାତ୍ରା ଆରମ୍ଭ କରିଥିଲେ । “ଭାରତରେ ସ୍ୱରାଜ୍ୟ ନ ହେବା ପର୍ଯ୍ୟନ୍ତ ମୁଁ ଆଉ ଆଶ୍ରମକୁ ଫେରିବ ନାହିଁ । ସ୍ୱରାଜ୍ୟ ଆଣି ଫେରିବି, ନଚେତ୍ ମୋର ମୃତ ଶରୀରକୁ ସମୁଦ୍ରରେ ଭସାଇ ଦେବ ।” ଏହି କେତୋଟି ଶବ୍ଦ ଉଚ୍ଚାରଣ କରି ମହାତ୍ମାଜୀ ଆଶ୍ରମ ତ୍ୟାଗ କରି ଗଲାବେଳର ଦୃଶ୍ୟ କିପରି ମର୍ମସ୍ପର୍ଶୀ ତାହା ନ ଦେଖିଲା ବ୍ୟକ୍ତି ସହଜରେ ଅନୁଭବ କରିପାରିବ ନାହିଁ । ସେହି ଦୃଶ୍ୟ ଏବଂ ସେହି ଦିନର ଘଟଣାବଳୀ ମନେ ପଡ଼ିଲେ ମୋ ମନରେ ସ୍ୱତଃ ରାଜା ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ରଙ୍କର ବନବାସ କଥା ମନେ ପଡ଼େ । ରାଜା ରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର, ସୀତା ଓ ଲକ୍ଷ୍ମଣଙ୍କୁ ସଙ୍ଗରେ ଧରି ଚଉଦ ବର୍ଷ ବନବାସ କରିବାପାଇଁ ଅଯୋଧ୍ୟା ନଗରୀ ଯେଉଁଦିନ ତ୍ୟାଗ କରିଥିଲେ ସେତେବେଳର ଦୃଶ୍ୟ କିପରି ମର୍ମସ୍ପର୍ଶୀ ହୋଇଥିବ ତାହା କଳ୍ପନା କରିହେବ ନାହିଁ । ଅଗଣିତ ନରନାରୀଙ୍କ ହୃଦୟ ଅଧୀର, ଚକ୍ଷୁ ଲୋତକ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ଏବଂ ତାହାଙ୍କ ସହିତ ବନଗମନ କରିବାକୁ ସମସ୍ତଙ୍କର ଆକୁଳ ପ୍ରାର୍ଥନା । କିନ୍ତୁ ଶ୍ରୀରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ର ଧୀର ଓ ଗମ୍ଭୀର ଭାବରେ ଏସବୁ ଦ୍ୱାରା ପ୍ରଭାବିତ ନ ହୋଇ, ହସି ହସି ବନଗମନ କରିଥିଲେ ।
ମହାତ୍ମାଜୀ ଆଶ୍ରମ ପରିତ୍ୟାଗ କଲାବେଳେ ଉଚ୍ଚାରଣ କରିଥିବା ମନ୍ତ୍ରର ଉଦ୍ଦେଶ୍ୟ କ’ଣ ?
(1) ଜୀବନକୁ ସ୍ୱ ସ୍ୱାର୍ଥରେ ବିନିଯୋଗ କରିବା ।
(2) ମୋକ୍ଷପ୍ରାପ୍ତି ହେବା ।
(3) ଲକ୍ଷ୍ୟ ସାଧନ ନିମନ୍ତେ ସକଳ ପ୍ରତିବନ୍ଧକ ସତ୍ତ୍ୱେ ଲକ୍ଷ୍ୟପଥରୁ ବିଚ୍ୟୁତ ନ ହେବା ।
(4) ନିଜର ବ୍ୟାକୁଳ ଭାବ ପ୍ରକାଶ କରିବା ।
= 3
~ The mantra 'ମନ୍ତ୍ରଂ ବା ସାଧୟେତ୍ ଶରୀରଂ ବା ପାତୟେତ୍' means 'I will either accomplish my aim or let my body fall'. Its purpose is to stay on the path to the goal whatever the obstacles (C). The passage shows this through Gandhi's vow on leaving Sabarmati Ashram: he would not return without swaraj, and if he failed, his dead body should be cast into the sea. Why not the others: (A) using life for self-interest is the opposite, since he staked his life for the nation; (B) moksha or spiritual liberation is never mentioned; (D) he was showing firm resolve, not anxiety. Word help: ସାଧୟେତ୍ = should accomplish, ପାତୟେତ୍ = should let fall, ଦୀକ୍ଷିତ = initiated. Context: the 'great undertaking' is the Dandi March (Salt Satyagraha), which actually began on 12 March 1930 (the passage prints October); Gandhi broke the salt law at Dandi on 6 April 1930. In Odisha, satyagrahis led by Gopabandhu Choudhury and Acharya Harihar Das marched from Cuttack to Inchudi in Balasore district and broke the salt law there in April 1930. The same spirit returned in Gandhi's 'Do or Die' call during the 1942 Quit India Movement.

Q 192 | odi | Literature & Comprehension
(Passage as in Q191.)
ଭାରତରେ ମହତ୍ କର୍ମାନୁଷ୍ଠାନ ଗଢ଼ା ହେବା ପଛରେ କେଉଁ ମହନୀୟ ଉଦ୍ଦେଶ୍ୟ ପ୍ରକାଶ ପାଇଛି ?
(1) ଭାରତର ଗୌରବ ବୃଦ୍ଧି କରିବା
(2) ଲୁପ୍ତ ଗୌରବକୁ ମନେ ପକାଇବା
(3) ସୁପ୍ତ ଶକ୍ତିର ଜାଗରଣ ଓ ପରାଧୀନତାର ଅନ୍ଧକାର ଦୂର କରି ସ୍ୱାଧୀନତାର ଆଲୋକ ପ୍ରଦାନ
(4) ଭାରତର ଯୁବପିଢ଼ିଙ୍କୁ ଏକତ୍ର କରିବା
= 3
~ Option C — awakening India's dormant strength and removing the darkness of subjection to give the light of freedom — is the purpose the passage states. Its opening lines say that through this great undertaking (the Salt Satyagraha of 1930) India's sleeping power (ସୁପ୍ତ ଶକ୍ତି) woke up, and the rays of its lost glory gave a new light to wipe out forever the blackness of subjection (ପରାଧୀନତାର କାଳିମା) from the hearts of crores of Indians. Why not the others: (A) 'increasing India's glory' is not said — the glory had been lost and only shone out again; (B) 'remembering lost glory' picks up a single phrase, while the passage's point is freedom, not memory; (D) uniting the youth is not mentioned at all. Word help: ସୁପ୍ତ = asleep, dormant; ରଶ୍ମିରେଖା = ray of light; ପ୍ରତିଭାତ = shone out; କାଳିମା = blackness, stain; ଅପସାରିତ = removed. Comprehension tip: choose the option that covers the whole idea of the sentence, not one that merely repeats an attractive phrase from it — options built from the passage's own words, like (B), are a common trap.

Q 193 | odi | Literature & Comprehension
(Passage as in Q191.)
ମହାତ୍ମାଜୀଙ୍କ ସାବରମତୀ ଆଶ୍ରମ ତ୍ୟାଗର ଦୃଶ୍ୟ କ’ଣ ସ୍ମରଣ କରାଇ ଦିଏ ?
(1) ଗାନ୍ଧିଜୀଙ୍କ ପ୍ରଚଣ୍ଡ ପରାକ୍ରମୀଗୁଣକୁ
(2) ଶ୍ରୀରାମଚନ୍ଦ୍ରଙ୍କର ବନବାସ ଘଟଣା
(3) ଗାନ୍ଧିଜୀଙ୍କ ନୈରାଶ୍ୟତା
(4) ଶ୍ରୀରାମଙ୍କ ଅଯୋଧ୍ୟାକୁ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟାବର୍ତ୍ତନ କଥା
= 2
~ The scene of Gandhi leaving Sabarmati Ashram reminds the writer of Sri Ramachandra's exile (ବନବାସ) — option B. The passage says plainly that whenever he recalls that scene and that day's events, the story of King Ramachandra going into exile comes to his mind on its own (ସ୍ୱତଃ). The comparison is apt: both left home for a hard duty — Rama for fourteen years in the forest with Sita and Lakshmana, Gandhi for a march with no promise of return — and in both cases the crowds were moved while the leader stayed calm. Why not the others: (A) Gandhi's 'fierce valour' is not what the scene recalls; (C) despair (ନୈରାଶ୍ୟତା) is wrong, since he set out full of resolve; (D) is the trap — it speaks of Rama's return (ପ୍ରତ୍ୟାବର୍ତ୍ତନ) to Ayodhya, a joyful homecoming, whereas the passage compares a departure. Context: Gandhi set out from Sabarmati on 12 March 1930 with 78 companions for Dandi, about 385 km away on the Gujarat coast. Tip: in such questions, check the direction of the event — leaving or returning — before you choose.

Q 194 | odi | Literature & Comprehension
(Passage as in Q191.)
ଶ୍ରୀରାମଙ୍କର ଅଯୋଧ୍ୟା ତ୍ୟାଗ କେଉଁ ଗୁଣର ଶିକ୍ଷା ପ୍ରଦାନ କରେ ?
(1) ଶ୍ରୀରାମଙ୍କ ଅହଂକାରୀଗୁଣ
(2) ବୀରତ୍ୱ ଓ ପରାକ୍ରମ
(3) ପିତୃଭକ୍ତି
(4) ଦୁଃଖ ଯନ୍ତ୍ରଣା ସତ୍ତ୍ୱେ ଧୀର ଓ ଗମ୍ଭୀର ଭାବରେ ପରିସ୍ଥିତିର ସମ୍ମୁଖୀନ
= 4
~ Sri Rama's leaving Ayodhya teaches us to face a situation calmly and gravely in spite of grief and pain — option D. The passage stresses that countless men and women were restless and in tears and begged to go with him, yet Sri Ramachandra, 'ଧୀର ଓ ଗମ୍ଭୀର ଭାବରେ', was not swayed and went to the forest smiling (ହସି ହସି). That composure under pressure is the quality highlighted, and the writer sees the same steadiness in Gandhi leaving Sabarmati. Why not the others: (A) arrogance (ଅହଂକାର) is the opposite of the calm described; (B) valour and might are not discussed in this scene; (C) filial devotion (ପିତୃଭକ୍ତି) is the trap — in the Ramayana Rama went into exile to honour his father Dasharatha's promise to Kaikeyi, so the idea is true of the story, but the passage does not mention it at all. Comprehension tip: answer only from what the passage says, not from what you know of the story elsewhere. Word help: ଧୀର = calm, patient; ଗମ୍ଭୀର = grave, composed; ଅଧୀର = restless; ଲୋତକ = tears.

Q 195 | odi | Literature & Comprehension
(Passage as in Q191.)
ଶ୍ରୀରାମଙ୍କ ବନ ଗମନରେ ତତ୍କାଳୀନ ପରିବେଶ କିଭଳି ଥିଲା?
(1) ପ୍ରଜାକୁଳ ପ୍ରସନ୍ନ ଥିଲେ
(2) ନରନାରୀଙ୍କ ହୃଦୟ ଅଧୀର ଚକ୍ଷୁ ଲୋତକାପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ
(3) ବଡ଼ ଗମ୍ଭୀର
(4) ଆକାଶ ମେଘାଚ୍ଛନ୍ନ
= 2
~ The mood at the time of Sri Rama's departure is given in the passage itself: 'ଅଗଣିତ ନରନାରୀଙ୍କ ହୃଦୟ ଅଧୀର, ଚକ୍ଷୁ ଲୋତକ ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ' — the hearts of countless men and women were restless and their eyes full of tears, and all of them pleaded to go to the forest with him. That matches option B. Why not the others: (A) says the people were pleased (ପ୍ରସନ୍ନ), the exact opposite of the grief described; (C) 'ବଡ଼ ଗମ୍ଭୀର' is a trap built from the passage's own word, but there ଗମ୍ଭୀର describes Rama's own manner (ଧୀର ଓ ଗମ୍ଭୀର ଭାବରେ), not the surroundings; (D) a cloudy sky is never mentioned. Word help: ଅଧୀର = restless (ଅ + ଧୀର, the opposite of calm); ଲୋତକ = tears; ଆକୁଳ ପ୍ରାର୍ଥନା = desperate plea; ବନଗମନ = going to the forest. Comprehension tip: when a question asks about the atmosphere or surroundings (ପରିବେଶ), look at how the crowd and the place are described, and keep that separate from how the main character behaves. The contrast — weeping people, calm Rama — is the heart of the passage and mirrors Gandhi's departure from Sabarmati.

Q 196 | odi | Literature & Comprehension
ନିର୍ଦ୍ଦେଶ (ପ୍ରଶ୍ନସଂଖ୍ୟା 196 ରୁ 200) : ଅନୁଚ୍ଛେଦଟିକୁ ସାବଧାନତା ସହକାରେ ପଢ଼ି ନିମ୍ନରେ ଦିଆଯାଇଥିବା ସଠିକ୍ ଉତ୍ତରଟି ବାଛ ।
ଯୋଗକୁ ହେଉ ବା ଦୁର୍ଯୋଗକୁ ହେଉ କୁରୁକୁଳର ଆଚାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଦ୍ରୋଣ ଓ ପ୍ରତ୍ୟାଖ୍ୟାତ ଏକଲବ୍ୟ ମଧ୍ୟରେ ପୁଣିଥରେ ସାକ୍ଷାତ ହୋଇଛି । ଗୁରୁଙ୍କୁ ଚକିତ କରି ଏକଲବ୍ୟ ତା’ର ବିଦ୍ୟାର ପରିଚୟ ଦେଇଛି । ଅସବର୍ଣ୍ଣ ବା ଅପାଂକ୍ତେୟ ହେଲେ ମଧ୍ୟ ଜଣେ ବିଦ୍ୟାକାମୀ ଆପଣାର ଶ୍ରଦ୍ଧା ଏବଂ ବିଶ୍ୱାସ ବଳରେ କିପରି ପରଂପରାଗତ ସକଳ ନିୟନ୍ତ୍ରଣକୁ ଲଂଘନ କରିଯାଇପାରେ, ଦ୍ରୋଣାଚାର୍ଯ୍ୟଙ୍କୁ ଏକଲବ୍ୟ ତାହାରି ପରିଚୟ ଦେଇଛି । ଯେକୌଣସି ଆଚାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଏଥିରେ ଗୌରବାନ୍ୱିତ ଅନୁଭବ କରିବାର କଥା । ଦ୍ରୋଣାଚାର୍ଯ୍ୟଙ୍କର ହୃଦୟ ଅସଲ ଆଚାର୍ଯ୍ୟଙ୍କର ହୃଦୟ ହୋଇଥିଲେ ଦ୍ରୋଣ ସେଦିନ ଏକଲବ୍ୟକୁ କୁଣ୍ଢାଇ ଧରିଥାନ୍ତେ। ଗୁରୁ, ଛାତ୍ରଠାରୁ ଶିଖିଥାନ୍ତା, ପରମ୍ପରାର ନିର୍ମୋକ ଖସି ପଡ଼ିଥାନ୍ତା; ଦ୍ରୋଣାଚାର୍ଯ୍ୟଙ୍କର ଭିତରେ ଥିବା ଅସଲ ଗୁରୁ ସେଦିନ ନବଜନ୍ମ ଲାଭ କରିଥାଆନ୍ତା । କିନ୍ତୁ ମହାଭାରତକାର ବ୍ୟାସଦେବଙ୍କର କାବ୍ୟ ଦୃଷ୍ଟିରେ ହୁଏତ ଏତକ ସାହସ ନଥିଲା । ସାମାଜିକ ପ୍ରେୟ ଉପରେ ମାନବିକ (ଆଧ୍ୟାତ୍ମିକ ?) ଶ୍ରେୟର ବିପୁଳତାକୁ ଚିହ୍ନି ନେବା ଲାଗି ସାହିତ୍ୟ ଦୃଷ୍ଟିରେ ଯେଉଁ ସାହସ ରହିବା ଦରକାର, ବ୍ୟାସଦେବଙ୍କର ହୁଏତ ସେହି ସାହସ ନଥିଲା । ତେଣୁ ଏକଲବ୍ୟର ସାହସ ତାଙ୍କୁ ପ୍ରଧାନତଃ ଦୁଃସାହସ ବୋଲି ହିଁ ମନେହେଲା ଓ ସେ ଏହି ଦୁଃସାହସର ଶାସ୍ତି ଦେବାକୁ ଏକ ସାହିତ୍ୟିକ ନ୍ୟାୟ ଖୋଜି ବସିଲେ । ଏହି ଶାସ୍ତିକୁ ଏକ ଆବଶ୍ୟକତା ବୋଲି ଦେଖାଇବା ଉଦ୍ଦେଶ୍ୟରେ ଦ୍ରୋଣାଚାର୍ଯ୍ୟଙ୍କୁ ଆଚାର୍ଯ୍ୟର ଆସନରୁ ତଳକୁ ଓହ୍ଲାଇ ଆଣିବାକୁ ପଡ଼ିଲା । ତେବେ ଯାଇ ଏକଲବ୍ୟ କଥା ସମ୍ପୂର୍ଣ୍ଣ ହେଲା । କୌଳିକତା ଜିତିଲା, କୁଳୀନତା ଜିତିଲା ; କିନ୍ତୁ ଆଚାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ପଦର ଅପମାନ କରାଗଲା ।
ଆଚାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଦ୍ରୋଣ ଏବଂ ଏକଲବ୍ୟଙ୍କର ପୁନଃ ସାକ୍ଷାତରେ କ’ଣ ହୋଇଛି ?
(1) ଦ୍ରୋଣାଚାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଏକଲବ୍ୟଙ୍କୁ ଇର୍ଷା କରିଛନ୍ତି ।
(2) ଗୁରୁଙ୍କ ସହିତ ଏକଲବ୍ୟଙ୍କର ଦ୍ୱନ୍ଦ୍ୱ ହୋଇଛି ।
(3) ଆଶ୍ଚର୍ଯ୍ୟଜନକ ଭାବେ ଗୁରୁଙ୍କୁ ସ୍ୱବିଦ୍ୟାର ପରିଚୟ ଦେଇଛି
(4) ଗୁରୁଙ୍କର ନିଜ ଶିଷ୍ୟ ପ୍ରତି ଅହେତୁକ ଶ୍ରଦ୍ଧା ଜାଗ୍ରତ ହୋଇଛି
= 3
~ At their second meeting, Eklavya astonished his guru by showing his own learning — option C. Right after saying that Drona and the rejected (ପ୍ରତ୍ୟାଖ୍ୟାତ) Eklavya met again, the passage states: 'ଗୁରୁଙ୍କୁ ଚକିତ କରି ଏକଲବ୍ୟ ତା'ର ବିଦ୍ୟାର ପରିଚୟ ଦେଇଛି' (ଚକିତ = astonished). Why not the others: (A) Drona's envy is only implied later by his conduct; it is not what the passage says happened at the meeting; (B) there was no quarrel or conflict (ଦ୍ୱନ୍ଦ୍ୱ) between them; (D) no reverence for the pupil awoke in the guru — the writer laments that Drona failed to embrace him. Background from the Mahabharata (Adi Parva): Drona turned Eklavya away because he was a Nishada and Drona taught only the Kuru princes; Eklavya made a clay image of Drona, practised archery before it and became a master archer. When Drona found out, he asked for Eklavya's right thumb as gurudakshina, and Eklavya gave it. Word help: ଅସବର୍ଣ୍ଣ = of a different varna; ଅପାଂକ୍ତେୟ = one not allowed to sit in the same row at a meal, an outcast. Tip: for 'what happened' questions, look for the sentence that directly follows the event in the passage.

Q 197 | odi | Literature & Comprehension
(Passage as in Q196.)
କେଉଁଥିପାଇଁ ଆଚାର୍ଯ୍ୟ ଗୌରାବାନ୍ୱିତ ହେବା କଥା ?
(1) ଶିଷ୍ୟର ଦକ୍ଷତାର ପରିଚୟ ପାଇବା
